\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{k}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}}{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

For
k=2
and
j=0,1,2
, we have

(4.17)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\Big|\partial_{Y}^{2}&A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)\Big|\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-4}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}\Big|e^{-\frac{2}{3}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))^{\frac{3}{2}}-(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))^{\frac{3}{2}})}\Big|\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-3}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}^{2}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}\Big|e^{-\frac{2}{3}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))^{\frac{3}{2}}-(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))^{\frac{3}{2}})}\Big|\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-4}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-3}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}^{2}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.\end{split}

By Lemma
4.1
, we know that for any
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}\cup\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{2}\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq C(1+|Y-Y_{c}|)^{-\frac{4}{3}}\leq C|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|,\quad|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}},

which along with (
4.17
) implies

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{2}

\displaystyle A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-4}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Similarly, we can show that for any
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}\cup\mathcal{N}^{-}
and
0\leq Z\leq Y
,

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{2}A_{2}(Z)A_{1}(j,Y)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-4}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Summing up, we conclude that for
k,j=0,1,2
and
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}\cup\mathcal{N}^{-}
with
0\leq Z\leq Y
,

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{k}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}}{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},

and

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{2}(Z)A_{1}(j,Y)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}\frac{|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{k}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}}{|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|^{j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{j}{2}}}e^{-\gamma_{1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Case 3.
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}
.

By Lemma
A.4
,
A.3
and (
A.1
) again, for
k=0,1,2
and
j=0,1,2
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{k}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))^{\frac{3}{2}}}|.

Since
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
and
|\kappa\eta_{i}^{La}|<\delta_{0}
, there exists
\gamma_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))^{\frac{3}{2}}}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\gamma_{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

This shows that for
k=0,1,2
and
j=0,1,2
,

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{k}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}e^{-\gamma_{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{3}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{3}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}-|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{3}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{4}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

By a similar argument, for
k,j=0,1,2
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{2}(Z)A_{1}(j,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{4}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Case 4.
Y\in\mathcal{N}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
.

By Lemma
A.4
,
A.3
and (
A.1
), for
k=0,1,2
and
j=0,1,2
, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)\Big|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-j}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{j}{2}}e^{-\gamma_{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

On the other hand, by Lemma
4.1
, for any
Z\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\leq(1+|Z-Y_{c}|)^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq C|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}.

This shows that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(j,Z)\Big|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{4}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Similarly, for
k,j=0,1,2
, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\partial_{Y}^{k}A_{2}(Z)A_{1}(j,Y)\Big|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{j-2-k}{3}}e^{-\gamma_{4}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

We conclude our lemma by summing up the above four cases.
∎

4.3.
Approximate solution to the Airy equation

Let
w_{app}
and
\psi_{app}
be defined as (
4.9
) and (
4.10
). Then
w_{app}
and
\psi_{app}
satisfy

(4.18)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{app}-(U_{s}-c)w_{app}=F+Err_{1}w_{app}+Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{app},\\
&\partial_{Y}(A(Y)^{-1}\partial_{Y})\psi_{app}(Y)=w_{app}(Y),\quad\quad\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{app}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{app}(Y)=0.\end{split}

Now we show the estimates of
w_{app}
with the source term
F\sim(U_{s}-c)^{-1}
in the sublayer.

Proposition 4.5
.

Let
\delta_{0}>0
be a constant in Lemma
A.1
and
c_{i}\geq c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Suppose
-\delta_{0}<\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta^{La})<\delta_{0}
,
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{1}
and
e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\in L^{\infty}
. Then it holds that

(1)

For
0<\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+|\varepsilon||e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Moreover, for
0<c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
, we have

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}(Y)w_{app}(Y)|+

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(2)

For
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Before presenting the proof, we introduce some notations and estimates used frequently. For any
Y\geq 0
, we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)=\big\{Z\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}:|\kappa(\eta^{La}(Z)-\eta^{La}(Y))|\leq 1\big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)=\big\{Z\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}:|\kappa(\eta^{La}(Z)-\eta^{La}(Y))|>1\big\},

where the definitions of
\mathcal{N}^{+}
and
\mathcal{N}^{-}
are given in (
4.12
)-(
4.13
).

For
Z,Y\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle e^{\vartheta|Y-Z|}\leq Ce^{C|\eta_{r}^{La}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}-\eta_{r}^{La}(Z)^{\frac{3}{2}}|}\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{C|\eta_{r}^{La}(Y)-\eta_{r}^{La}(Z)|(|\eta_{r}^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\eta_{r}^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},

which implies

(4.19)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}e^{\vartheta|Y-Z|}\\
&\quad\leq e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})(1+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\\
&\quad\leq e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.\end{split}

The proof of Proposition
4.5
is rather long and split into the following three subsections.

4.3.1.
Estimates of
w_{app}(Y)

We consider three cases.

Case 1.
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
.
In this case, we first notice that

(4.20)

\displaystyle|w_{app}(Y)|\sim|(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|.

Thus, it is enough to show the control of
w_{app}(Y)
. By the definition of
w_{app}
and Lemma
4.4
, for any
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
, we have

(4.21)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|w_{app}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
=&|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}).\end{split}

For
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)
and
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
, we get by Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{6}},

and

(4.22)

\displaystyle e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\leq e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|},

which along with (
4.19
) show that

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{6}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{6}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Z}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

where in the last step we used the fact that

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|}dZ\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
, we get by Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle C\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq C\kappa^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}},

and

(4.23)

\displaystyle e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\leq e^{-C(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},

from which, we infer that

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|e^{\vartheta Z}F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}|e^{\vartheta Z}(U_{s}(Z)-c)F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{6}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|e^{\vartheta Z}(U_{s}(Z)-c)F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{N}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}

for any
N>0
.

For
Z\in\mathcal{N}
and any
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
, we get by Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\kappa|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{12}},

and

(4.24)

\displaystyle e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\leq

\displaystyle e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}},

from which, we infer that

\displaystyle|I_{3}|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{12}}e^{-\vartheta Y}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{N})}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{12}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}|\log|c_{i}|\|(u-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{N})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{N}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

for any
N>0
. Therefore, we conclude that for
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
4.20
) implies that for any
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
,

(4.25)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Case 2.
|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1
and
|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L
.
We denote
\mathcal{N}_{1}=\{Y\geq 0:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1,|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
. Notice that
\mathcal{N}_{1}
is a subset of
\mathcal{N}^{+}\cup\mathcal{N}^{-}
and
\mathcal{N}\subset\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
. By Lemma
4.1
, we know that for any
|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L
,
|\eta^{La}(Y)|\sim|U_{s}-c|
.
Again by Lemma
4.4
, we deduce that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
,

(4.26)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\\
&\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad+C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad+C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&=I_{4}+I_{5}+I_{6}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)
, we get by Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}},

and

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta(Z)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},

from which and (
4.19
), we infer that

(4.27)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{4}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta(Z)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ\\
\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{7}{4}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Then we get by (
4.19
) and (
4.23
) that

(4.28)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{5}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{7}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ\\
\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}
,
|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|\geq 1
. Then we infer that

\displaystyle|I_{6}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{7}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{N})}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\log c_{i}|,

which along with (
4.26
), (
4.27
) and (
4.28
) deduces that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
,

(4.29)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-&c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Case 3.
|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq 3M+1
.
We denote
\mathcal{N}_{2}=\{Y:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq 3M+1\}
. By Lemma
4.4
, we have that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{2}
,

(4.30)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(U_{s}-&c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\\
\leq&C\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&+C\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|F(Z)|dZ=I_{7}+I_{8}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{2}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle{C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}},}

which along with (
4.19
) and (
4.23
) gives

(4.31)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{7}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ\\
\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{2}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)
, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle|I_{8}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\log c_{i}|,

which along with (
4.30
) and (
4.31
) implies that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{2}

(4.32)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-&c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Summing up the above three cases, we conclude the following estimate

(4.33)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

4.3.2.
Estimates of
\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)
and
\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{app}

We consider three cases.

Case 1.
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
.

Again in this case, it is enough to show the control of
\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)
. By Lemma
4.4
, we have

(4.34)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\Bigg(\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}+\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}\Bigg)|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
=&|\varepsilon|^{-1}(II_{1}+II_{2}+II_{3}).\end{split}

For any
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)
, we get by Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq C,

and for
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{5}{12}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}),

and for
Z\in\mathcal{N}
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\leq Ce^{-|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}}.

Then we apply (
4.19
), (
4.22
), (
4.23
) to obtain

(4.35)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|II_{1}|\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)||\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

and

(4.36)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|II_{2}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{5}{12}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})e^{-C(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dZ\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{N}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle|II_{3}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}\int_{\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{N}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\log c_{i}|,

for any
N>0
,
which along with (
4.34
) and (
4.35
) deduce that for any
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
,

(4.37)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\log c_{i}|.

Case 2.
|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1
and
|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L
.
By Lemma
4.4
, for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
,

(4.38)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\\
&\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\Bigg(\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}+\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}\Bigg)|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ\\
&\qquad+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ=|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(II_{4}+II_{5}+II_{6}).\end{split}

For
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}},

and for
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)
,

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{5}{4}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}),

and for
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
and
Z\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{5}{4}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|\geq 1.

Then we apply (
4.19
), (
4.23
) and a similar process as in
I_{4}-I_{6}
to obtain

(4.39)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|II_{4}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|}dZ\\
\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

and

(4.40)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|II_{5}|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{5}{4}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})e^{-C(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dZ\\
\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle|II_{6}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{5}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}|\log c_{i}|,

which along with (
4.38
) deduce that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}_{1}
,

(4.41)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Case 3.
|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq 3M+1
.
By Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|F(Z)|dZ.

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
4.37
) and (
4.41
) deduces the following estimate

(4.42)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Using the equation of
w_{app}
in (
4.18
) and along with estimates (
4.33
) and (
4.42
), it is easy to see that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon||e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{app}|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

By Lemma
4.3
, (
4.25
), (
4.29
) and (
4.32
), and using the fact
|U_{s}-c|\geq c_{i}\geq c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}(Y)w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Moreover, by Lemma
4.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

4.3.3.
Estimates of
\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)
and
\psi_{app}(Y)

Here we restrict that
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
. By the definition of
\psi_{app}(Y)
, we know that

\displaystyle A^{-1}\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)=-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}w_{app}(Z)dZ,

from which, we get by the formula of
w_{app}
and integration by parts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}A^{-1}\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)=&A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)(\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ\\
&+A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A_{1}(Z)(\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ\\
&+\int_{Y}^{+\infty}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)](\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ.\end{split}

Therefore, we get by (
2.6
) that

(4.43)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(U_{s}(Y)-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq&\Bigg(\left|(U_{s}-c)A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)(\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|\\
&+\left|(U_{s}-c)A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A_{1}(Z)(\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|\Bigg)\\
&+\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)](\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z))^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|\\
=&H_{1}+H_{2}.\end{split}

Case 1.
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
. As in the same argument in
I_{1}-I_{3}
, we have

(4.44)

\displaystyle\begin{split}H_{1}\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\Bigg(\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}+\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}\Bigg)|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\\
&\qquad\times|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-1}||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|U_{s}-c|^{-1}dZ\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

where we used (
4.22
), (
4.23
) and

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-1}||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y),

and

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-1}||\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}),\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y),

and

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|U_{s}-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\quad\leq Ce^{-|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}-c|^{-1}dZ\leq C|\varepsilon|^{N}.

For
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
, we have

\displaystyle H_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-2}|F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}(\{|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L\})}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\vartheta(Z-Y)}dZ\leq Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
4.43
) and (
4.44
) implies that for
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}(Y)-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Case 2.
|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1
and
|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L
.
In this case, using the fact
|U_{s}-c|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|
, we get

(4.45)

\displaystyle\begin{split}H_{1}\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\qquad\times\Bigg(\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}+\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}\Bigg)|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dZ\\
&\qquad+Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ\\
\leq&C|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

where we used the facts that

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}\leq

\displaystyle C\kappa|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}},\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y),

and

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}(1+|\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}),\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y),

and
|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|\geq 1
so that

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ\leq C|\log c_{i}|.

Notice that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}\cup\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
,

\displaystyle H_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{Y}^{+\infty}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-2}|F(Z)|dZ\leq Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

and for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1,Y\leq Y_{c}\}\cup\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
,

\displaystyle H_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+C|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-2}|F(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Thus, we conclude that for any
Y\in\big\{Y:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 3M+1\}\cup\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\big\}
,

\displaystyle H_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
4.43
) and (
4.45
) shows that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq 3M+1\}\cup\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}(Y)-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Case 3.
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq 3M+1\}
.
A similar argument as in Case 2 shows that

(4.46)

\displaystyle\begin{split}H_{1}\leq&Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\{Z:|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|\geq 3M+2\}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)|^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ\\
&+Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}-c|^{-1}dZ\\
\leq&C|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Notice that

\displaystyle H_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{Y_{+}}^{+\infty}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|(U_{s}-c)\int_{\mathcal{N}}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}F(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathcal{N}}|U_{s}(Z)-c|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
4.43
) and (
4.46
) implies that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}(Y)-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|e^{-\vartheta Y}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Summing up, we conclude that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(4.47)

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}(Y)-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

By (
4.47
) and
\psi_{app}=-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Z)dZ
, we can obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

In a similar way as in Proposition
4.5
, we can show that

Proposition 4.6
.

Let
\delta_{0}>0
be a constant in Lemma
A.1
and
c_{i}\geq c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Suppose
-\delta_{0}<\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta^{La})<\delta_{0}
,
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{1}
and
e^{\vartheta Y}F\in L^{\infty}
. Then it holds that

(1)

For
0<\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)w_{app}(Y)|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|+|\varepsilon||e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Moreover, for
0<c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
, we have

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}Err_{1}(Y)w_{app}(Y)|+|e^{\vartheta Y}Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(2)

For
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|+|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi_{app}(Y)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

4.4.
Non-homogeneous Airy equation

In this subsection, we construct a solution to the non-homogeneous Airy equation:

(4.48)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w-(U_{s}-c)w=F,\quad\Lambda\psi(Y)=w(Y),\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w(Y)=0.\end{split}

We introduce the notation

\displaystyle Airy[\cdot]=\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})-(U_{s}-c),\quad\Lambda=\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y})-\alpha^{2}.

Proposition 4.7
.

Let
\delta_{0}>0
be a constant in Lemma
A.1
and
c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
. Suppose
-\delta_{0}<\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta^{La})<\delta_{0}
and
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{1}
. Then it holds that

•

Take
\theta>0
. For
F
such that
e^{\vartheta Y}F\in L^{\infty}
, there exists a solution
(w,\psi)
to (
4.48
) satisfying

(1)

for
0<\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial^{2}_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)w\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(2)

for
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(U_{s}-c)e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

•

Take
\theta>0
. For
F
such that
e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\in L^{\infty}
, there exists a solution
(w,\psi)
to (
4.48
) satisfying

(1)

for
0<\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
:

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(2)

for
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Remark 4.8
.

The results in Proposition
4.7
are also valid when we replace
w
by
\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi
according to the relation
\Lambda\psi=\omega.

Proof.

Since the proof for the above two kinds of source terms
F
is similar, we only show the harder case, i.e.,
F\sim(U_{s}-c)^{-1}
.
We shall construct the solution
(w,\psi)
to (
4.48
) via the iteration.

For given source term
F
with
e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\in L^{\infty}
, we define
w^{(0)}(Y)=w_{app}(Y)
, where
w_{app}(Y)\in W^{2,\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+})
is the solution to

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{app}-(U_{s}-c)w_{app}=F+Err_{1}w_{app}+Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{app}

constructed in Proposition
4.5
. Then for any
j\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we define that
w^{(j)}(Y)
is the solution constructed in Proposition
4.5
with the source term
-Err_{1}w^{(j-1)}(Y)-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w^{(j-1)}(Y)
.
According to Proposition
4.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\log c_{i}|(\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},\quad j=1,\\
&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|(\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w^{(j-2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w^{(j-2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}),\quad j\geq 2.\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore, for any
j\geq 1
, we take
|\varepsilon|
small enough so that
|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\leq\frac{1}{2}
to obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\right)^{j}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C2^{-j}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Now we define

\displaystyle w(Y)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}w^{(j)}(Y).

From the above results, we infer that

(4.49)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
\quad&\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
\quad&\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Now we turn to construct
\psi(Y)
. Here we restrict
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
. We first recall that

\displaystyle\psi_{app}(Y)=\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A(Y^{\prime})\int_{Y^{\prime}}^{+\infty}w_{app}(Z)dZdY^{\prime}.

By Proposition
4.5
, we know that

(4.50)

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi_{app}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{app}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\Lambda\psi_{app}(Y)=w(Y)+(w_{app}(Y)-w(Y))-\alpha^{2}\psi_{app}(Y).

Hence, to construct
\psi(Y)
, we need to shrink the error:
(w_{app}(Y)-w(Y))-\alpha^{2}\psi_{app}(Y)
. For this purpose, we define

\displaystyle\psi^{(1)}(Y)=\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A(Y^{\prime})\int_{Y^{\prime}}^{+\infty}(w_{app}(Z)-w(Z))-\alpha^{2}\psi_{app}(Z)dZdY^{\prime},

from which, we directly have

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}(Y)=-A(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}(w_{app}(Z)-w(Z))-\alpha^{2}\psi_{app}(Z)dZ.

Therefore, by the proof of (
4.49
) and (
4.50
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

which implies

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Now for any
j\geq 2
, we define

\displaystyle\psi^{(j)}(Y)=-\alpha^{2}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A(Y^{\prime})\int_{Y^{\prime}}^{+\infty}\psi^{(j-1)}(Z)dZdY^{\prime}.

Then we obtain that for any
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\alpha|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\alpha|^{2(j-1)}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Then we define

\displaystyle\psi(Y)=\psi_{app}(Y)+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\psi^{(j)}.

From the above arguments, we infer that
\Lambda\psi(Y)=w(Y)
and

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(1+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}},

and

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(\psi-\psi_{app})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}(\psi-\psi_{app})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|e^{\vartheta Y}F\|_{L^{\infty}}.

The proof is completed.
∎

4.5.
Homogeneous Airy equation

In this subsection, we construct a non-trivial solution to the homogeneous Airy equation:

(4.51)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a}-(U_{s}-c)w_{a}=0,\quad\Lambda\psi_{a}(Y)=w_{a}(Y),\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{a}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{a}(Y)=0.\end{split}

We denote

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,Y)=-A(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y)=-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,Z)dZ.

Then we define

(4.52)

\displaystyle w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)=\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))}{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))},\quad\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)=\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y)}{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))},

so that
\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y})\psi_{a}^{(0)}=w_{a}^{(0)}
. By (
4.7
), we know that

\displaystyle Airy[w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)]=Err_{1}(Y)w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)+Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w^{(0)}_{a}(Y).

To eliminate the above errors, we define
w_{a,err}(Y)
to be a solution constructed in Proposition
4.7
with source term
-Err_{1}(Y)w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)-Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w^{(0)}_{a}(Y)
.

Proposition 4.9
.

Let
\delta_{0}>0
be a constant in Lemma
A.1
and
c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
.
Suppose that
-\delta_{0}<\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta^{La})<\delta_{0}
and
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{1}
. Then there exists a solution
\psi_{a}(Y)\in W^{4,+\infty}
to (
4.51
) such that for any
0<\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}),

and for
1\lesssim\vartheta\leq 3\eta_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}).

Moreover, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\psi_{a}(0)}{\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)}=

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{O}(|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})).

Proof.

By the definition of
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y)
and the properties of Airy function, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y})\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)=w_{a}^{(0)}(Y),

and

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a}^{(0)}-(U_{s}-c)w_{a}^{(0)}=Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}+Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}.

To construct a solution to (
4.51
), we need to construct a solution
(w_{a,err},\psi_{a,err})=(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)},\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})
to the following equation:

(4.53)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a,err}-(U_{s}-c)w_{a,err}=-Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)},\\
&\Lambda\psi_{a,err}(Y)=w_{a,err}(Y)+\alpha^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y),\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{a,err}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{a,err}(Y)=0.\end{split}

Notice that for
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\sim|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}|\partial_{Y}^{k}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))|,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2-k}{3}}|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2-k,Y)Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{5\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))|.

Due to
0\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
, we get by Lemma
4.4
that for any
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(0)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2-k}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-(2-k)}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(0)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

Moreover, by Lemma
A.2
and
A.5
, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{-1}\sim|\varepsilon||c|^{-1},

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}|c|^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

With the above estimates in hand, we can show the estimates of the solution to (
4.53
). Let us first show the control of
Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)
and
Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)
. For any
Y-Y_{c}\geq L
, we first notice that

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}Y}\leq Ce^{-L|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\vartheta Y},\quad k=0,1,

which along with Lemma
4.3
and
c_{i}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
implies that for any
Y-Y_{c}\geq L
,

(4.54)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-L|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\vartheta Y},\\
&|(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-L|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\vartheta Y}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}\cap\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}},\quad k=0,1.

Then by Lemma
4.3
, for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}\cap\{Y:|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L\}
, we have

(4.55)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{2}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|e^{-\vartheta Y},\end{split}

and

(4.56)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-\vartheta Y}.\end{split}

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}
,
|U_{s}-c|\sim|\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq|\eta^{La}(0)|
. For any
Y\in(\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N})\cap\{Y:|Y|\geq|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\}
, we have that for
k=0,1

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Therefore, we obtain

(4.57)

\displaystyle|(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(0)|^{2}|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{2}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|,

and

(4.58)

\displaystyle|(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\eta^{La}(0)|e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|.

Summing up (
4.54
), (
4.55
), (
4.56
), (
4.57
) and (
4.58
), we conclude that for any
|Y|\geq|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-\vartheta Y},\\
&|(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-\vartheta Y}.\end{split}

For
Y\in[0,|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}]
, we have

\displaystyle|(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|c|^{2}e^{-\vartheta Y},

\displaystyle|(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|c|e^{-\vartheta Y}.

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2}),

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|).

Then it follows from Proposition
4.7
that

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\log c_{i}|(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}),

and

\displaystyle\|e^{\vartheta Y}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\vartheta Y}\psi_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}).

In particular, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{a}(0)-\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}})\ll\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0),

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)-\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\leq C|c|^{-1}|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|+|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}})\ll\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0).

Thus, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\psi_{a}(0)}{\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)}=

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{O}(|\log c_{i}|^{2}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2}+|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})).

This completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.
The Orr-Sommerfeld equation

To solve the quasi-incompressible system (
2.1
), we solve the following Orr-Sommerfeld(OS) type equation with general source term
F
:

(5.1)

\displaystyle\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-(U_{s}-c)\Lambda\phi+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\phi=F.

We introduce the operators

\displaystyle OS[\cdot]=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})-(U_{s}-c)\Lambda+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s}),

\displaystyle Ray[\cdot]=

\displaystyle(U_{s}-c)\Lambda-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s}),

\displaystyle Airy[\cdot]=

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})-(U_{s}-c).

We develop an Airy-Airy-Rayleigh iteration to constructe the solution to the Orr-Sommerfeld equation (
5.1
). More precisely, we start our iteration with

\displaystyle\phi^{(1)}=\psi^{(1,0)}+\psi^{(1,1)}+\varphi^{(1)},

where
\psi^{1,0},\psi^{(1,1)}
and
\varphi^{(1)}
solve the following equations respectively,

\displaystyle Airy[\Lambda\psi^{(1,0)}]=F,

\displaystyle Airy[\Lambda\psi^{(1,1)}]=-\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi^{(1,0)}-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi^{(1,0)},

\displaystyle Ray[\varphi^{(1)}]=\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi^{(1,1)}+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi^{(1,1)}.

Then we obtain

\displaystyle OS[\phi^{(1)}]=OS[\psi^{(1,0)}]+OS[\psi^{(1,1)}]+OS[\varphi^{(1)}]=\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(1)}+F.

Now we define the iteration scheme inductively. For any
j\geq 1
,

\phi^{(j+1)}=\psi^{(j+1,0)}+\psi^{(j+1,1)}+\varphi^{(j+1)},

where
\psi^{(j+1,0)},\psi^{(j+1,1)}
and
\varphi^{(j+1)}
solve

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Airy[\Lambda\psi^{(j+1,0)}]=-\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(j)},\\
&Airy[\Lambda\psi^{(j+1,1)}]=-\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi^{(j+1,0)}-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi^{(j+1,0)}\\
&Ray[\varphi^{(j+1)}]=\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi^{(j+1,1)}+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi^{(j+1,1)}.\end{split}

Then we have

\displaystyle OS\Big[\sum_{j=1}^{N}\phi^{(j)}\Big]=\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(N)}+F.

Formally,
\phi=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\phi^{(j)}
satisfies (
5.1
).

Remark 5.1
.

Compared with the Airy-Rayleigh iteration for the construction of the solution to the classical Orr-Sommerfeld equation in the incompressible case, the Airy-Airy-Rayleigh iteration is the key to avoid the loss of derivatives caused by the commutator
[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]
.

We introduce the following functional spaces

(5.2)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{X}}=

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

(5.3)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{Y}}=

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

where

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}=\|e^{\eta Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}},

for a fixed constant
\eta\in(0,\eta_{0})
. We also denote

\displaystyle\|f\|_{W^{s,\infty}_{\eta}}=\sum_{k=0}^{s}\|\partial_{Y}^{k}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

It is easy to see that

(5.4)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

In this section, we focus on the following regime:
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
with

(5.5)

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}=\mathbb{H}_{1}\cap\big\{(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}:|\alpha|\sim|c|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq c_{i}\leq c_{1}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\text{ with }0<c_{0}<c_{1}\ll 1\big\}.

For any
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
, we have

(5.6)

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq\frac{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}{c_{i}}|U_{s}-c|\leq C|U_{s}-c|,\quad Y\geq 0,

5.1.
Non-homogeneous OS equation

Let us first show some key estimates related to the Airy-Airy-Rayleigh iteration. For convenience, we define

(5.7)

\displaystyle Airy[\Lambda\psi]=F_{\psi},

(5.8)

\displaystyle Airy[\Lambda\widetilde{\psi}]=-\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi=F_{\widetilde{\psi}},

(5.9)

\displaystyle Ray[\varphi]=\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\widetilde{\psi}+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\widetilde{\psi}=F_{\varphi}.

Then it holds that

\displaystyle OS[\psi+\widetilde{\psi}+\varphi]=F_{\psi}+\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi.

Lemma 5.2
.

For any
(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\in L^{\infty}_{\eta}
, there exists a solution
(\psi,\widetilde{\psi},\varphi)\in\mathcal{X}^{2}\times\mathcal{Y}
to (
5.7
)-(
5.9
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\|\varphi\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Proof.

According to Proposition
4.7
, we have

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

(5.10)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\widetilde{\psi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

By the definition of
F_{\widetilde{\psi}}
in (
5.8
) and

(5.11)

\displaystyle[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]f=

\displaystyle-2\partial_{Y}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{3}f-3\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{2}f-\partial_{Y}^{3}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}f,

we obtain

(5.12)

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\widetilde{\psi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{3}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{3}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}},

from which and (
5.10
), we infer that

(5.13)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Next we show the existence and estimates of
\varphi
. By Proposition
3.2
and a similar argument as in (
5.12
), we have

\displaystyle\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\log c_{i}||\varepsilon|\Big(\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\Big)+C|\log c_{i}|\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|\Big(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+c_{i}^{-2}\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+c_{i}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\Big)+C|\log c_{i}|\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\log c_{i}|\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{3}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}},

where we used (
5.6
) and (
5.13
).

To estimate
\partial_{Y}^{3}\varphi
, we first take
\partial_{Y}
to the commutator
[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]f
and get by (
5.11
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]f=

\displaystyle-2\partial_{Y}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{4}f-5\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{3}f-4\partial_{Y}^{3}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{2}f-\partial_{Y}^{4}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}f,

which gives

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}\big(\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\widetilde{\psi}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}\|(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\psi},\widetilde{\psi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|c_{i}|^{-1}+|c_{i}|^{-2}+|c_{i}|^{-1})\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

Then by Proposition
3.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{3}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

We turn to the estimate of
\partial_{Y}^{4}\varphi
. Taking
\partial_{Y}^{2}
to the commutator
[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]f
, we get by (
5.11
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]f=

\displaystyle-2\partial_{Y}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{5}f-7\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{4}f-9\partial_{Y}^{3}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{3}f

\displaystyle-5\partial_{Y}^{4}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{2}f-\partial_{Y}^{5}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}f,

which along with (
5.6
) implies that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\big(\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\widetilde{\psi}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{4},\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}c_{i}^{-1}+c_{i}^{-2}+c_{i}^{-1})\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

In order to estimate
\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}
, we show the control of
|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\widetilde{\psi}}
firstly. A direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}F_{\widetilde{\psi}}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{Y}\big(\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\partial_{Y}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{4}\psi+5\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{3}\psi+4\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{3}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{4}(A^{-1})\partial_{Y}\psi

\displaystyle-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\partial_{Y}\psi-\partial_{Y}^{2}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi,

which implies

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\widetilde{\psi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{4}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}+|c_{i}|^{-1}+1)\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Therefore, using the equation of
\widetilde{\psi}
in (
5.8
), the above estimate along with (
5.13
) gives

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\widetilde{\psi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{4}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}\Lambda\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\Lambda\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\widetilde{\psi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

Finally, we apply Proposition
3.2
to obtain

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\widetilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\psi}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

This shows that

\displaystyle\|\varphi\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\psi\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

∎

Now we are in a position to construct the solution to the Orr-Sommerfeld equation (
5.1
).

Proposition 5.3
.

For any
F
with
(U_{s}-c)F\in L^{\infty}_{\eta}
, there exists a solution
\phi\in\mathcal{X}
to (
5.1
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Proof.

We use Airy-Airy-rayleigh iteration introduced at the beginning of this section. For
j=1
, let
(\psi^{(1,0)},\psi^{(1,1)},\varphi^{(1)})
be the solution of the system (
5.7
)-(
5.9
) with

\displaystyle F_{\psi}=F_{\psi^{(1,0)}}=F.

For
j\geq 2
, let
(\psi^{(j,0)},\psi^{(j,1)},\varphi^{(j)})
be the solution of the system (
5.7
)-(
5.9
) with

\displaystyle F_{\psi}=F_{\psi^{(j,0)}}=-\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(j-1)}.

Then we define

\displaystyle\phi^{(j)}=\psi^{(j,0)}+\psi^{(j,1)}+\varphi^{(j)},\quad j\geq 1.

It follows from (
5.6
) that for
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi^{(j,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{4},\partial_{Y}^{3})\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|\alpha^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y},\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}},

from which and Lemma
5.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(j,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}},

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(j,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}},

\displaystyle\|\varphi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

These estimates along with (
5.4
) show that for
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(j,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\psi^{(j,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\varphi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(j-1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-1}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

By the smallness assumptions on
\alpha,~|c|,~|\varepsilon|
, which guarantee
|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\|\phi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-1}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

By Lemma
5.2
again, we have

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

which yield that

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\psi^{(1,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Then
\phi=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\phi^{(j)}
is the solution to (
5.1
) with

\displaystyle\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\|\phi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

∎

5.2.
Homogenous OS equation

We first construct the slow mode
\phi_{s}
to the homogeneous OS equation
OS[\phi]=0
around the solution
\varphi_{Ray}
constructed in Proposition
3.5
to the homogeneous Rayleigh equation. From
\phi_{s}
we can construct a slow solution to the homogeneous quasi-incompressible system (
2.1
).
According to Proposition
3.5
, we know that

\displaystyle\varphi_{Ray}=\Big(\frac{1-m^{2}(1-c)^{2}}{(1-c)^{2}}\Big)(U_{s}-c)e^{-\beta Y}+\varphi_{Ray,R}

with

(5.14)

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|.

Putting
\varphi_{Ray}
into the operator
OS[\cdot]
, we obtain

\displaystyle OS[\varphi_{Ray}]=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray}=\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,0}+\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,R},

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{Ray,0}=\Big(\frac{1-m^{2}(1-c)^{2}}{(1-c)^{2}}\Big)(U_{s}-c)e^{-\beta Y}.

Lemma 5.4
.

There exists a solution
\phi_{s}\in W^{4,\infty}
to
OS[\phi]=0
satisfying

\displaystyle\phi_{s}=

\displaystyle\varphi_{Ray}+\widetilde{\phi}_{s},

where

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Proof.

Let
(\psi^{(1,0)},\psi^{(1,0)},\varphi^{(1)})
be the solution of the system (
5.7
)-(
5.9
) with

\displaystyle F_{\psi}=F_{\psi^{(1,0)}}=-\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray}.

By (
5.6
) and (
5.14
), we have

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi^{(1,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{4},\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\alpha||\log c_{i}|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|.

Then we get by Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{3},
