\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5},

\displaystyle\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Let
\phi^{(1)}=\psi^{(1,0)}+\psi^{(1,1)}+\varphi^{(1)}
, which holds(by (
5.6
))

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1,0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\psi^{(1,1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5},

and

\displaystyle OS[\varphi_{Ray}+\phi^{(1)}]=\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(1)}.

Let
\phi^{(2)}
be the solution of
OS[\phi]=F_{\psi}
with

\displaystyle F_{\psi}=F_{\psi^{(2,0)}}=-\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(1)}.

By Proposition
5.3
, we get

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

We define
\widetilde{\phi}_{s}=\phi^{(1)}+\phi^{(2)}
. By the smallness assumptions on
\alpha,~|c|,~|\varepsilon|
, which guarantee
|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

(5.15)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\phi^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Then
\phi_{s}=\varphi_{Ray}+\widetilde{\phi}_{s}
is a solution to
OS[\phi]=0
.
∎

Next we construct the fast mode
\phi_{f}
to the homogeneous OS equation, which is a perturbation of the solution
\psi_{a}
to the homogeneous Airy equation constructed in Proposition
4.9
. From
\phi_{f}
we can construct a fast solution to the homogeneous quasi-incompressible system (
2.1
)
By Proposition
4.9
, the main part of
\psi_{a}
is given by
\psi_{a}^{(0)}
, which is defined by the Airy functions as (
4.52
).

According to the properties of Airy function(see also the proof of Proposition
4.9
), we have the following estimates:
due to
0\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
, it holds that for any
Y\geq 0

(5.16)

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{k}{3}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(0)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},\quad k=0,1,2,3,4,

which along with (
4.19
) implies that for any
s\geq 0
,

(5.17)

\displaystyle|e^{\eta Y}(U_{s}-c)^{s}\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{k}{3}+\frac{s}{3}},\quad Y\geq 0.

We know that

\displaystyle OS[\psi_{a}]=\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{a}+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}.

So, we will construct
\widetilde{\phi}_{f}
satisfying

\displaystyle OS[\widetilde{\phi}_{f}]=-\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{a}-\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}.

Lemma 5.5
.

There exists a solution
\phi_{f}\in W^{4,\infty}
to
OS[\phi]=0
satisfying

\displaystyle\phi_{f}=

\displaystyle\psi_{a}+\widetilde{\phi}_{f},

where

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

and

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\phi}_{f}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+\alpha)|\log c_{i}|^{3}\ll|\psi_{a}(0)|,

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\phi}_{f}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{3}\ll|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)|.

Proof.

We define
\psi_{f}^{(0)}
to be the solution to the following Airy equation:

(5.18)

\displaystyle Airy[\Lambda\psi_{f}^{(0)}]=-\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{a}=F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}.

Using Proposition
4.9
and (
5.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\big(\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{3}\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}})

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|(\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{3}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}.

Then by Proposition
4.7
(2), we get

(5.19)

\displaystyle\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{2}\|(U_{s}-c)F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}.

In the following, we define
\varphi_{f}^{(0)}=\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}+\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}+\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}
, where
\varphi_{f}^{(0,k)},~k=0,1,2
is the solution to the following Rayleigh equation respectively:

\displaystyle Ray[\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}]+\alpha^{2}(U_{s}-c)\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}=\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}=F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}},

\displaystyle Ray[\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}]=\alpha^{2}(U_{s}-c)\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}=F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}},

\displaystyle{Ray[\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}]=\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})+\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{f}^{(0)}+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{f}^{(0)}=F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}.}

According to (
5.17
) and the fact
|\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})|\leq Ce^{-\eta_{0}Y}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C\|\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C\|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C.

Since the estimates in Proposition
3.2
are also valid for
\alpha=0
in the definition of
Ray[\cdot]
, we obtain

(5.20)

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

Next we give a more precise estimate for
\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}(0)
, which has the formula:

\displaystyle\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}(0)=

\displaystyle-c\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{A(Y)}{(U_{s}-c)^{2}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}dY^{\prime}dY

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-c\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}\frac{A(Y)}{(U_{s}-c)^{2}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}dY^{\prime}dY

\displaystyle-c\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}\frac{A(Y)}{(U_{s}-c)^{2}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}dY^{\prime}dY=I_{1}+I_{2}.

Due to
|\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})|\leq Ce^{-\eta_{0}Y}
and
|U_{s}-c|\geq C^{-1}>0
, we get by (
5.16
) that

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\eta_{0}Y_{0}}\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}|e^{\eta_{0}Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}|dY

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(0)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dY,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|.

For
I_{2}
, we apply Lemma
3.1
with
G(Y)=A(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}dY^{\prime}
and
Y=0
to infer that

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle C|G(0)|+C|c||\log c_{i}|(\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Thus, we focus on the estimates of
G
and
\partial_{Y}G
. As in the estimate of
I_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle|G(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{+\infty}|e^{\eta_{0}Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}|dY

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta(Y)-\eta(0)|(|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}dY\leq C|\varepsilon|,

and

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}G|\leq C|A\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{a}^{(0)}|+C|A^{\prime}(0)|\int_{0}^{+\infty}|e^{\eta_{0}Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}|dY\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+C|\varepsilon|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq C|\varepsilon|+C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|)|\log c_{i}|.

This along with the estimate of
I_{1}
shows that

(5.21)

\displaystyle|\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|)|\log c_{i}|\ll|\psi_{a}(0)|.

For
\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}
, we get by Proposition
3.2
that

(5.22)

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,1)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\alpha^{2}(|\log c_{i}|\|\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\alpha^{2}|\log c_{i}|\|\varphi_{f}^{(0,0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C\alpha^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{2}.

For
\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}
, we first give the estimate of
F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}
. By Proposition
4.9
, we have

\displaystyle\|F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\varepsilon[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|\|(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|,

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial^{2}_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\partial_{Y}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{4}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(\partial_{Y},1)\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y},1)(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}[\Lambda,\partial_{Y}^{2}]\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y},1)\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{2}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{4},\partial_{Y}^{3})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y},1)\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{2}+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}.

By using the equation of
\psi_{f}^{(0)}
in (
5.18
), we have

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{5}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{4}\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}\Lambda\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\Lambda\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}F_{\psi_{f}^{(0)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\varepsilon(U_{s}-c)^{2}\partial_{Y}^{4}\psi_{a}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}\|(U_{s}-c)(\partial_{Y}^{3},\partial_{Y}^{2},\partial_{Y})\psi_{a}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}.

Thus, we conclude that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|,

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{2}+C\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{2},

from which and Proposition
3.2
, we infer that

(5.23)

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|\|F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}F_{\varphi_{f}^{(0,2)}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{2}.

Summing up (
5.20
), (
5.22
) and (
5.23
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

This along with (
5.21
) implies that

(5.24)

\displaystyle|\varphi_{f}^{(0)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|\ll|\psi_{a}(0)|,

(5.25)

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\varphi_{f}^{(0)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|\ll|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)|.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle OS[\phi_{f}^{(0)}+\varphi_{f}^{(0)}]=\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{f}^{(0)}.

Let
\phi^{(1)}_{f}
be the solution of
OS[\phi]=F_{\psi}
with

\displaystyle F_{\psi}=-\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(0)}_{f}.

By Proposition
5.3
, we get

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(1)}_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}.

We define

(5.26)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\phi}_{f}=\psi_{f}^{(0)}+\varphi_{f}^{(0)}+\phi^{(1)}_{f},

which holds

(5.27)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq\|\psi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}+\|\varphi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}+C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|\varphi_{f}^{(0)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

This along with (
5.24
) and (
5.25
) implies that

(5.28)

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\phi}_{f}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{3}\ll|\psi_{a}(0)|,

(5.29)

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\phi}_{f}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{3}\ll|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)|.

Thus,
\phi_{f}=\psi_{a}+\widetilde{\phi}_{f}
is a solution of
OS[\phi_{f}]=0
.
∎

6.
The quasi-incompressible system

In this section, we solve the following quasi-incompressible system:

(6.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho+\mathrm{i}\alpha u+\partial_{Y}v=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})[u+(U_{s}-c)\rho]-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)u-v\partial_{Y}U_{s}-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{-2}\rho=f_{u},\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)v-m^{-2}\partial_{Y}\rho=f_{v}.\end{aligned}\right.

As we mentioned in Section 2, the above system mainly corresponds to the incompressible part of the system (
1.3
). Moreover, the slow and fast solutions to the system (
6.1
) constructed in this section are the main part of the slow and fast solutions to the system (
1.3
).

We denote by
\mathcal{L}_{Q}
the operator on the left hand side of (
6.1
). We introduce the stream function
\phi(Y)
defined by

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\phi(Y)=u+(U_{s}-c)\rho,\quad-\mathrm{i}\alpha\phi(Y)=v.

Then the quasi-incompressible system can be reduced to the following Orr-Sommerfeld type system:

(6.2)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\varepsilon\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-(U_{s}-c)\Lambda\phi+\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\phi=\Omega(f_{u},f_{v}),\\
&\rho=m^{2}A^{-1}(Y)\left(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi+\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi-\frac{f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\right),\end{aligned}\right.

where

(6.3)

\displaystyle\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})=\frac{1}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}f_{u})-f_{v}.

Let us give some formulas about the density and its derivatives. First of all, we know that

(6.4)

\displaystyle\rho=m^{2}A^{-1}\Big(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi+\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi-\frac{f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\Big).

Then with
\phi
and
\rho
, we can recover the velocity
u,v
by

(6.5)

\displaystyle u=\partial_{Y}\phi(Y)-(U_{s}-c)\rho,\quad v=-\mathrm{i}\alpha\phi.

Taking
\partial_{Y}
to the equation (
6.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\rho=-A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\rho+m^{2}A^{-1}\Big(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi+\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\phi-\frac{\partial_{Y}f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\Big).

On the other hand, we may write

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi=\varepsilon A\Lambda(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi+A\varepsilon\alpha^{2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi+\varepsilon A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\partial_{Y}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi,

\displaystyle(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi=(U_{s}-c)(A\Lambda\phi+A\alpha^{2}\phi+A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\partial_{Y}\phi),

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\phi=A\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s})\phi+A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi.

Then we find that

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi+\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\phi

\displaystyle=A\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})+A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A(m^{-2}A\rho+\frac{f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha})+A\varepsilon\alpha^{2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-A(U_{s}-c)\alpha^{2}\phi

\displaystyle=A\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})+m^{-2}\partial_{Y}A\rho+A\varepsilon\alpha^{2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-A(U_{s}-c)\alpha^{2}\phi+A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\frac{f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha},

from which and (
6.3
)-(
6.4
), we infer that

(6.6)

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\rho=

\displaystyle-A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\rho+m^{2}A^{-1}\Big(A\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})+m^{-2}\partial_{Y}A\rho

\displaystyle+A\varepsilon\alpha^{2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-A(U_{s}-c)\alpha^{2}\phi-\frac{\partial_{Y}f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}+A^{-1}\partial_{Y}A\frac{f_{u}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})+m^{2}\alpha^{2}(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-(U_{s}-c)\phi)-\frac{m^{2}}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}f_{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}\alpha^{2}(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi-(U_{s}-c)\phi)-m^{2}f_{v},

(6.7)

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho=

\displaystyle m^{2}\alpha^{2}(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi-\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi)-m^{2}\partial_{Y}f_{v}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}A\rho-2m^{2}\alpha^{2}\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi-m^{2}\partial_{Y}f_{v}-\mathrm{i}m^{2}\alpha f_{u}.

Our goal of this section is as follows.

•

Construct a decay solution
(\rho,u,v)
to the quasi-incompressible system (
6.1
) with decay source term by solving the non-homogenous Orr-Sommerfeld system (
6.2
). Such solution and its estimates will be used in the iteration during the construction of the solutions to the linearized CNS (
1.3
).

•

Construct two linearly independent decay solutions
(\rho_{s},u_{s},v_{s})
and
(\rho_{f},u_{f},v_{f})
to (
6.1
), whose stream functions
\phi_{s}
and
\phi_{f}
are the slow mode and fast mode to the Orr-Sommerfeld equation respectively. These two solutions are the main part of two linearly independent solution to (
1.3
), from which we can obtain the dispersion relation.

In this section, we focus on the regime:
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
defined by (
5.5
).

6.1.
Non-homogeneous quasi-incompressible system

In this subsection, we solve the non-homogeneous quasi-incompressible system via solving the OS system (
6.2
).

Proposition 6.1
.

For any
f_{u}\in W^{1,\infty}_{\eta}
and
f_{v}\in L^{\infty}_{\eta}
, there exists a solution
(\rho,u,v)\in W^{2,\infty}_{\eta}
to (
6.1
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

and

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Proof.

According to (
6.4
), we have

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|c_{i}^{-1}\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{3}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C(1+|\varepsilon||\alpha|^{2}c_{i}^{-1})\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

where we used Proposition
5.3
in the last step.

Similarly, by (
6.6
) and (
6.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|(U_{s}-c)f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Thanks to the relation (
6.5
), we can deduce from the above estimates and Proposition
5.3
that

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq Cc_{i}^{-1}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{3}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}+C\|(\rho,\partial_{Y}\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

and

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\alpha|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial^{2}_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\partial^{2}_{Y}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\phi\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Thanks to
\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)=-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho
, we finally obtain

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|\|(\rho,(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\alpha|\|(\rho,\partial_{Y}\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

The proof is completed.
∎

6.2.
Homogeneous quasi-incompressible system

In this subsection, we construct two linearly independent solutions
(\rho_{s},u_{s},v_{s})
(slow solution) and
(\rho_{f},u_{f},v_{f})
(fast solution) to the homogeneous quasi-incompressible system via constructing the slow mode
\phi_{s}
and fast mode
\phi_{f}
to the homogeneous OS equation.

Proposition 6.2
.

Let
\beta=\beta_{r}+i\beta_{i}
be given by (
3.13
). Then there exists a slow solution
(\rho_{s},u_{s},v_{s})
and a fast solution
(\rho_{f},u_{f},v_{f})
belonging to
W^{2,\infty}_{\beta_{r}}
of the homogeneous quasi-incompressible system satisfying

•

For the density
\rho_{s}
and
\rho_{f}
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha||\rho_{s}|+|\partial_{Y}\rho_{s}|+|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{s}|\leq C|\alpha|^{2}e^{-\beta_{r}Y},

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

•

For the velocity, we have

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2},

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

•

For the boundary value of the velocity, we have the asymptotic expansions:

\displaystyle u_{s}(0)=

\displaystyle(1-m^{2})U_{s}^{\prime}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}),

\displaystyle v_{s}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha(-(1-m^{2})c+\frac{\beta}{U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})}+\mathcal{O}((|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|^{2}+|c|^{2})|\log c_{i}|^{4})),

\displaystyle u_{f}(0)=

\displaystyle=\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{3}),

\displaystyle v_{f}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha(\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{3})).

Proof.

Recall that the slow mode
\phi_{s}
of the OS equation constructed in Lemma
5.4
satisfies

\displaystyle\phi_{s}=\varphi_{Ray}+\widetilde{\phi}_{s}=\varphi_{Ray,0}+\varphi_{Ray,R}+\widetilde{\phi}_{s},

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{Ray,0}=\frac{A_{\infty}}{(1-c)^{2}}(U_{s}-c)e^{-\beta Y},

and
\varphi_{Ray,R}
and
\widetilde{\phi}_{s}
hold the estimates in (
5.14
) and (
5.15
).
We denote

\displaystyle\rho(\phi)=m^{2}A^{-1}\Big(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi+\partial_{Y}U_{s}\phi\Big).

Using the fact that

\displaystyle-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\varphi_{Ray,0}+\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\varphi_{Ray,0}=-\beta^{2}(U_{s}-c)\varphi_{Ray,0}-2\beta\partial_{Y}U_{s}\varphi_{Ray,0},

we have

\displaystyle|\rho(\varphi_{Ray,0})|=

\displaystyle\Big|m^{2}A^{-1}\Big(\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\varphi_{Ray,0}-(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\varphi_{Ray,0}+\partial_{Y}U_{s}\varphi_{Ray,0}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|e^{-\beta_{r}Y},

and by (
5.14
) and (
5.15
),

\displaystyle\|\rho(\varphi_{Ray,R})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|\rho(\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Then we conclude that

(6.8)

\displaystyle|\rho_{s}|=|\rho(\phi_{s})|\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|e^{-\beta_{r}Y}.

Using the formula (
6.6
),
OS[\phi_{s}]=0

(f_{u}=f_{v}=0)
and a similar argument as above, we deduce that

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{r}Y}|\partial_{Y}\rho(\phi_{s})|\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}e^{\beta_{r}Y}(|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray,0}|+|(U_{s}-c)\varphi_{Ray,0}|)+C|\alpha|^{2}(\|\varphi_{Ray,R}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}+\|\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}+C|\alpha|^{3}|\log c_{i}|+C|\alpha|^{3}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5},

which gives

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\rho_{s}|=|\partial_{Y}\rho(\phi_{s})|\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}e^{-\beta_{r}Y}.

Similarly, by (
6.7
) and (
6.8
), we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{s}|\leq C|\alpha|^{2}e^{-\beta_{r}Y}.

Next we give the estimate of
\rho_{f}=\rho(\phi_{f})
. By the construction of
\phi_{f}
in Lemma
5.5
, we know that

\displaystyle\phi_{f}=\psi_{a}+\widetilde{\phi}_{f},

where
\psi_{a}
is the solution to the homogeneous Airy equation satisfying

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

and
\widetilde{\phi}_{f}
given by (
5.26
) holds the estimate (
5.27
). So, we have

\displaystyle\|\phi_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

Using the formulas (
6.4
), (
6.6
) and (
6.7
) for
f_{u}=f_{v}=0
, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\|\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\partial_{Y}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\phi_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\big(\|\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|(U_{s}-c)\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C\alpha^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\big(\|\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\phi_{f}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

Using the relation

\displaystyle u=\partial_{Y}\phi-(U_{s}-c)\rho,\quad v=-\mathrm{i}\alpha\phi,

we get by Proposition
3.5
that

\displaystyle u_{s}(0)=

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\phi_{s}(0)+c\rho_{s}(0)=\partial_{Y}\varphi_{Ray}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4})+\mathcal{O}(|c||\alpha|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-m^{2})U_{s}^{\prime}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}),

\displaystyle v_{s}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\phi_{s}(0)=-\mathrm{i}|\alpha|\big(\varphi_{Ray}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\big(-(1-m^{2})c+\frac{\beta}{U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})}+\mathcal{O}((|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|^{2}+|c|^{2})|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big).

By using (
5.28
)-(
5.29
), we have

\displaystyle u_{f}(0)=

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\phi_{f}(0)+c\rho_{f}(0)=\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{3}),

\displaystyle v_{f}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\phi_{f}(0)=-\mathrm{i}\alpha\big(\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{3})\big).

By using the equation

\displaystyle\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)+\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho=0,

it is easy to derive that

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}U_{s}\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{s},v_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}U_{s}\partial_{Y}\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\rho_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|(\rho_{f},\partial_{Y}\rho_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{f},v_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha|\|(\rho_{f},\partial_{Y}\rho_{f},\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{f})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

The proof is completed.
∎

7.
The quasi-compressible system

To estimate the compressible part, we introduce the following quasi-compressible system:

(7.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho+\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})u+\mathrm{i}\alpha\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)u\\
&\qquad\quad-(\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{-2}+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s})\rho=q_{1},\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v+\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)v\\
&\qquad\quad-\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}vdY^{\prime}-m^{-2}\partial_{Y}\rho=q_{2}.\end{aligned}\right.

This system is used to reduce the error of the quasi-incompressible system (
6.1
) during an incompressible-compressible iteration process.

We introduce a new term
\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}vdY^{\prime}
in the third equation of (
7.1
), which is different from the Stokes system used in Yang and Zhang’s work
[
27
]
. With this term, we could decouple the equation of density, which is an elliptic equation. Moreover, we can derive the expression for density. This method could help us to construct spatial mode when
\alpha\in\mathbb{C}
.

We denote by
\mathcal{L}_{S}
the operator on the left hand side of (
7.1
).
In this section, we always assume that
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
with
\mathbb{H}
defined by (
5.5
) and
\beta=\beta_{r}+i\beta_{i}
given by (
3.13
).

Proposition 7.1
.

For any
q_{1},\partial_{Y}q_{2}\in L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}
, there exists a solution
(\rho,u,v)\in(W^{2,\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}})^{3}

to the system (
7.1
) satisfying

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|(U_{s}-c)u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-2}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|(q_{1},q_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

This proposition is a direct corollary of the following Proposition
7.3
and Proposition
7.4
.

We sketch our main ideas to construct the solution to the system (
7.1
).

(1)

Multiplying
\mathrm{i}\alpha
on both sides of the second equation in (
7.1
) and taking
\partial_{Y}
on the third equation of (
7.1
), we use the fact
\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)=\mathrm{i}\alpha u+\partial_{Y}v
to obtain

\displaystyle(1+\lambda)\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-m^{-2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\rho

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho=\mathrm{i}\alpha q_{1}+\partial_{Y}q_{2}.

Using the first equation in (
7.1
), the above equation can be written as

(7.2)

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}(1+\lambda)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho)

\displaystyle-\alpha^{2}(U_{s}-c)^{2}\rho-m^{-2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\rho

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho=\mathrm{i}\alpha q_{1}+\partial_{Y}q_{2}.

Then we apply the method of Green function and iteration to construct the density
\rho
to the equation (
7.2
).

(2)

We apply Proposition
4.7
to construct the tangent velocity
u
, since the second equation in (
7.1
) is in the form of Airy equation with source terms in terms of
q_{1},\rho
and
\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)
:

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})u-(U_{s}-c)u=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}q_{1}+m^{-2}\rho+\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s})\rho-\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{i}\alpha^{-1})q_{1}+\big(m^{-2}+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}+\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\big)\rho.

(3)

We can recover
\partial_{Y}v,\partial_{Y}^{2}v
from
\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)
and
u
. The vertical velocity
v
can be recovered from the last equation in (
7.1
). Indeed, we can write it as

(7.3)

\displaystyle(U_{s}-c)v+\int_{Y}^{\infty}\partial_{Y}(U_{s}-1)vdY^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v+\lambda\varepsilon\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-m^{-2}(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}\partial_{Y}\rho-(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}q_{2}.

For the left hand side of (
7.3
), we get by integration by parts that

\displaystyle(U_{s}-c)v+\int_{Y}^{\infty}\partial_{Y}(U_{s}-1)vdY^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(U_{s}-c)v-\int_{Y}^{\infty}(U_{s}-1)\partial_{Y}vdY^{\prime}-(U_{s}-1)v

\displaystyle=(1-c)v-\int_{Y}^{\infty}(U_{s}-1)\partial_{Y}vdY^{\prime}.

Then we may rewrite (
7.3
) as follows

\displaystyle(1-c+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})v=

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{\infty}(U_{s}-1)\partial_{Y}vdY^{\prime}+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}v+\lambda\varepsilon\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)

\displaystyle-m^{-2}(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}\partial_{Y}\rho-(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}q_{2},

in which all of the terms on the right hand side have been obtained in the previous steps.

7.1.
Solving the density equation

In this subsection, we solve the following density equation

(7.4)

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\mathrm{i}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}(1+\lambda)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho)&-\alpha^{2}(U_{s}-c)^{2}\rho-m^{-2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\rho\\
&-\mathrm{i}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho=\mathrm{i}\alpha q_{1}+\partial_{Y}q_{2}.\end{split}

Notice that

\displaystyle m^{-2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\rho+\alpha^{2}(U_{s}-c)^{2}\rho=

\displaystyle m^{-2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho-m^{-2}\alpha^{2}(1-m^{2}(U_{s}-c)^{2})\rho

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{-2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho-m^{-2}\alpha^{2}A(Y)\rho

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{-2}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\beta^{2})\rho-m^{-2}\alpha^{2}(A(Y)-A_{\infty})\rho.

Then we can rewrite (
7.4
) into the following form

(7.5)

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\beta^{2})\rho=&\alpha^{2}(A(Y)-A_{\infty})\rho-\mathrm{i}m^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}(1+\lambda)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho)\\
&-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}q_{1}-m^{2}\partial_{Y}q_{2}.\end{split}

We construct a decay solution
\rho
to (
7.5
) by the iteration. We first define

\displaystyle\rho^{(0)}(Y)=\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}F(q_{1},q_{2})(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}F(q_{1},q_{2})(Z)dZ\right),

where
F(q_{1},q_{2})(Y)=-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}q_{1}-m^{2}\partial_{Y}q_{2}
. According to the above definition, we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\beta^{2})\rho^{(0)}=-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}q_{1}-m^{2}\partial_{Y}q_{2}.

For
j\geq 0
, we define
\rho^{(j+1)}
as follows

\displaystyle\rho^{(j+1)}(Y)=\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}G_{j}(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}G_{j}(Z)dZ\right),

where

\displaystyle G_{j}(Z)=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}(A(Y)-A_{\infty})\rho^{(j)}_{st}+\Big(-\mathrm{i}m^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\nu}(1+\lambda)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho^{(j)})-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle G_{j,1}(Z)+G_{j,2}(Z).

Formally,
\rho(Y)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho^{(j)}(Y)
is a solution to (
7.5
).

We denote

\displaystyle f(Y)=\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}g(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}g(Z)dZ\right).

We show some useful esitmates about
f(Y)
in the following lemma.

Lemma 7.2
.

Let
\beta\in\mathbb{C}
with
\beta_{r}>0
and
|\beta_{i}|\leq C\beta_{r}
. Then there holds

•

For any
g(Y)\in L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\beta|^{2}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\beta|\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

•

For any
g(Y)\in L^{\infty}_{\eta}
with
\eta>2\beta_{r}
, we have

\displaystyle|\beta|\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq C\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Proof.

We first show the first statement of this lemma. By the definition of
f(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle|e^{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}Y}f(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\beta|^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta_{r}|Y-Z|}e^{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}|Y-Z|}|e^{\frac{\beta_{r}Z}{2}}g(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\beta|^{-2}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

By a direct calculation, we know that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}f(Y)=\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}g(Z)dZ-e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}g(Z)dZ\right).

Then we have

\displaystyle|e^{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}Y}\partial_{Y}f(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta_{r}|Y-Z|}e^{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}|Y-Z|}|e^{\frac{\beta_{r}Z}{2}}g(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\beta|^{-1}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

On the other hand, by using
\partial_{Y}^{2}f=\beta^{2}f+g
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

Now we turn to show the second statement. Again by the definition of
f
, we have

\displaystyle|e^{\beta_{r}Y}f(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle|\beta|^{-1}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta_{r}Z}|g(Z)|dZ+|\beta|^{-1}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{2\beta_{r}Z}|g(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\beta|^{-1}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{-(\eta-\beta_{r})Z}|e^{\eta Z}g(Z)|dZ+|\beta|^{-1}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-(\eta-2\beta_{r})Z}|e^{\eta Z}g(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\beta|^{-1}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq C\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

∎

Proposition 7.3
.

For any
q_{1},\partial_{Y}q_{2}\in L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}
, there exists a solution
\rho\in W^{2,\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}
satisfying

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

Proof.

We construct the solution
\rho
by the iteration. We define

\displaystyle\rho^{(0)}(Y)=\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}F(q_{1},q_{2})(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}F(q_{1},q_{2})(Z)dZ\right),

where
F(q_{1},q_{2})(Y)=-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{2}q_{1}-m^{2}\partial_{Y}q_{2}
. Hence, by Lemma
7.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

Recall that for any
j\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\rho^{(j+1)}(Y)=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}G_{j,1}(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}G_{j,1}(Z)dZ\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{-1}{2\beta}\left(e^{-\beta Y}\int_{0}^{Y}e^{\beta Z}G_{j,2}(Z)dZ+e^{\beta Y}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-\beta Z}G_{j,2}(Z)dZ\right),

where

\displaystyle\|G_{j,1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}},

\displaystyle\|G_{j,2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}).

Therefore, we apply Lemma
7.2
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C(|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}),

which implies

\displaystyle|\alpha|\|\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|\alpha|(|\alpha|\|\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}).

This finished the proof by performing the iteration due to the smallness of
|\alpha|
. ∎

7.2.
Constructing the velocity
(u,v)

In this subsection, we construct the velocity
(u,v)
to the system (
7.1
) once the density
\rho
is constructed in Proposition
7.3
.

Proposition 7.4
.

Let
\rho
be the density constructed in Proposition
7.3
for any given
q_{1},q_{2},\partial_{Y}q_{2}\in L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}
, there exists
u,v\in W^{2,\infty}_{\beta_{r}/2}
such that
(\rho,u,v)
is a solution to the system (
7.1
) satisfying

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|(U_{s}-c)u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-2}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|(q_{1},q_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

Proof.

By Proposition
7.3
, we know that

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

which along with the first equation in (
7.1
) deduces that

(7.6)

\displaystyle|\alpha|\sum_{k=0}^{2}\|\partial_{Y}^{k}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

Now we construct the tangent velocity
u
. Recall that
u
satisfies the following equation

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})u-(U_{s}-c)u=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{i}\alpha^{-1})q_{1}+(m^{-2}+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}+\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha(U_{s}-c))\rho.

By Proposition
4.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|(U_{s}-c)u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+C\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-2}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

Now we turn to show the control of
v
. Firstly, we notice that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}v=-\mathrm{i}\alpha(u+(U_{s}-c)\rho),

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}v=-\mathrm{i}\alpha\big(\partial_{Y}u+(U_{s}-c)\partial_{Y}\rho+U_{s}^{\prime}\rho\big).

Then we obtain

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

On the other hand, we know that
v
satisfies the following equation

\displaystyle(1-c+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})v=

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{\infty}(U_{s}-1)\partial_{Y}vdY^{\prime}+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}v+\lambda\varepsilon\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)

\displaystyle-m^{-2}(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}\partial_{Y}\rho-(\mathrm{i}\alpha)^{-1}q_{2},

which implies

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{Y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+C|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+C|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}q_{1},|\alpha|^{-1}q_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}.

The proof is completed.
∎

8.
The full linearized CNS system

In this section, we construct two linearly independent solutions to the homogeneous linearized CNS system:

(8.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho+\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})u+\mathrm{i}\alpha\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)u\\
&\qquad\qquad-(\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{-2}+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s})\rho-v\partial_{Y}U_{s}=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v+\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)v-m^{-2}\partial_{Y}\rho=0.\end{aligned}\right.

We denote by
\mathcal{L}
the linear operator of the above system. Recall that
\mathcal{L}_{Q}
and
\mathcal{L}_{S}
are the linear operators of the system (
6.1
) and the system (
7.1
) respectively.

We introduce main idea of the construction:

(1)

Let
(\rho_{os},u_{os},v_{os})
be either the slow or fast solution to homogeneous quasi-incompressible Navier-Stokes system constructed in Proposition
6.2
. By direct computations, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\rho_{os},u_{os},v_{os})=

\displaystyle(0,-\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho_{os})+\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os},v_{os})

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho_{os},\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os},v_{os}))

We denote
(\rho^{(1)}_{os},u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})=(\rho_{os},u_{os},v_{os})
. Then we introduce
(\rho^{(1)}_{st},u^{(1)}_{st},v^{(1)}_{st})
to be the solution to the system (
7.1
) constructed in Proposition
7.1
with the source term
(0,q^{(1)}_{1},q^{(1)}_{2})
:

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{1}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho^{(1)}_{os})-\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(1)}_{os}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})-\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(1)}_{os},

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{2}=

\displaystyle-\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os}).

Here
\sqrt{\nu}=|\varepsilon||\alpha|
. Then we define the initial step of the iteration by
(\rho^{(1)},u^{(1)},v^{(1)})=(\rho^{(1)}_{os},u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})+(\rho^{(1)}_{st},u^{(1)}_{st},v^{(1)}_{st})
. It is obvious that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\rho^{(1)},u^{(1)},v^{(1)})=\Big(0,-\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(1)}_{st},-\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(1)}_{st}dY^{\prime}\Big).

(2)

We define our iterative scheme inductively. For any
j\geq 1
, we define that
(\rho_{os}^{(j+1)},u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})
is the solution to the quasi-incompressible system (
6.1
) constructed in Proposition
6.1
with source term
(0,f^{(j)}_{u},f^{(j)}_{v})
, where
f^{(j)}_{u}=\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st},~f_{v}^{(j)}=\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st}dY^{\prime}
. According to such definition, we find that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{j}\rho^{(l)}+\rho^{(j+1)}_{os},

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{j}u^{(l)}+u^{(j+1)}_{os},\sum_{l=1}^{j}v^{(l)}+v_{os}^{(j+1)}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(0,-\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho_{os}^{(j+1)})+\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho_{os}^{(j+1)},\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\big).

Then as in the initial step, we use the system (
7.1
) to shrink the above errors. In details, we introduce
(\rho^{(j+1)}_{st},u^{(j+1)}_{st},v^{(j+1)}_{st})
to be the solution to the system (
7.1
) constructed in Proposition
7.1
with the source term
(0,q^{(j+1)}_{1},q^{(j+1)}_{2})
:

\displaystyle q^{(j+1)}_{1}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os})-i\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(j+1)}_{os},

\displaystyle q^{(j+1)}_{2}=

\displaystyle-\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os}).

Hence, we define the
(j+1)
th step of the iteration by
(\rho^{(j+1)},u^{(j+1)},v^{(j+1)})=(\rho^{(j+1)}_{os},u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os})+(\rho^{(j+1)}_{st},u^{(j+1)}_{st},v^{(j+1)}_{st})
. It is obvious that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{j+1}\rho^{(l)},\sum_{l=1}^{j+1}u^{(l)},\sum_{l=1}^{j+1}v^{(l)}\Big)=\Big(0,-\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j+1)}_{st},-\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j+1)}_{st}dY^{\prime}\Big).

(3)

By the above iteration, we formally obtain that
\mathcal{L}(\rho,u,v)=0
with

\displaystyle\rho=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\rho^{(j)},\quad u=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}u^{(j)},\quad v=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}v^{(j)}.

In this section, we always assume that
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
with
\mathbb{H}
defined by (
5.5
) and
\beta=\beta_{r}+\mathrm{i}\beta_{i}
be given by (
3.13
).
To realize the above iteration scheme rigorously, we introduce the space
\mathcal{Z}
for the density defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}=\big\{f\in W^{1,\infty}:\|f\|_{\mathcal{Z}}<+\infty\big\},

where

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{Z}}=\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

For the velocity, we take the space
\tilde{\mathcal{X}}
defined by

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{X}}=\big\{f\in W^{2,\infty}:\|f\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}<+\infty\big\},

where

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}=|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}.

We first give the estimates for the above iteration sequence.

Lemma 8.1
.

If
(\rho^{(j)}_{st},u^{(j)}_{st},v^{(j)}_{st})
belongs to
\mathcal{Z}\times\tilde{\mathcal{X}}^{2}
, then we have

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}},

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{st}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

Proof.

Recall that
(\rho_{os}^{(j+1)},u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})
is the solution to

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho_{os}+\mathrm{i}\alpha u_{os}+\partial_{Y}v_{os}=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})[u_{os}+(U_{s}-c)\rho_{os}]-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)u_{os}-v_{os}\partial_{Y}U_{s}-\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{-2}\rho_{os}=f_{u},\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v_{os}-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)v_{os}-m^{-2}\partial_{Y}\rho_{os}=f_{v},\end{aligned}\right.

with
f_{u}=\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st},~f_{v}=\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st}dY^{\prime}
.
By Proposition
6.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

and

\displaystyle\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\alpha|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}.

Here

\displaystyle f_{u}=\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st},\quad f_{v}=\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st}dY^{\prime},

\displaystyle\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})=\frac{1}{\mathrm{i}\alpha}\partial_{Y}(A^{-1}\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st})-\int_{Y}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\alpha\partial_{Y}U_{s}v^{(j)}_{st}dY^{\prime}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\|(U_{s}-c)\Omega(f_{u},f_{v})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\alpha|^{-1}\|f_{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|^{-1}\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}},

and

\displaystyle\|f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}f_{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\alpha|\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}}

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

and

\displaystyle\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}),

and

\displaystyle\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

and

\displaystyle\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

As a result, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\rho_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

\displaystyle\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

\displaystyle\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

This shows that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|v_{os}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

Now we turn to show the estimates about
(\rho_{st}^{(j+1)},u_{st}^{(j+1)},v_{st}^{j+1})
. By Proposition
7.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{2}\|\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\alpha|^{-1}\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-2}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C\|(q_{1},q_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\alpha|^{-1}\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}},

where

\displaystyle q_{1}=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os})-\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(j+1)}_{os},

\displaystyle q_{2}=

\displaystyle-\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(j+1)}_{os},v^{(j+1)}_{os}).

On the other hand, based on the estimates about
\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|q_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}+C|\varepsilon||\alpha|^{2}\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

\displaystyle\|q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}\leq C|\varepsilon||\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(j+1)},v_{os}^{(j+1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big),

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}\big(\|v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(j)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big).

Thus, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j+1)}_{st}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{st}^{(j+1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}.

∎

Now we construct the slow mode of the linearized CNS system (
8.1
).

Proposition 8.2
.

Let
(\rho_{s},u_{s},v_{s})
be the slow solution to the homogenous quasi-incompressible system constructed in Proposition
6.2
. Then there exists a slow solution
(\rho^{s},u^{s},v^{s})\in\mathcal{Z}\times\tilde{\mathcal{X}}\times\tilde{\mathcal{X}}
to the system (
8.1
) such that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\rho^{s}-\rho_{s}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u^{s}-u_{s}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+\|v^{s}-v_{s}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle u^{s}(0)=

\displaystyle(1-m^{2})U_{s}^{\prime}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\log c_{i}|^{4}),

\displaystyle v^{s}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\Big(-(1-m^{2})c+\frac{\beta}{U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})}+\mathcal{O}((|\alpha||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\alpha|^{2}+|c|^{2})|\log c_{i}|^{4})\Big).

Proof.

We define
(\rho^{(1)},u^{(1)},v^{(1)})=(\rho^{(1)}_{os}+\rho^{(1)}_{st},u^{(1)}_{os}+u^{(1)}_{st},v^{(1)}_{os}+u^{(1)}_{st})
, where
(\rho_{os}^{(1)},u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})=(\rho_{s},u_{s},v_{s})
and
(\rho_{st}^{(1)},u_{st}^{(1)},v_{st}^{(1)})
is the solution to the system (
7.1
) constructed in Proposition
7.1
with the source term
(0,q^{(1)}_{1},q^{(1)}_{2})
:

(8.2)

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{1}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})((U_{s}-c)\rho^{(1)}_{os})-\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(1)}_{os}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})-\mathrm{i}\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\alpha\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os})+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s}\rho^{(1)}_{os},

(8.3)

\displaystyle q^{(1)}_{2}=

\displaystyle-\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u^{(1)}_{os},v^{(1)}_{os}).

By Proposition
6.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|q_{1}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\varepsilon||\alpha|^{2}\|\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\rho^{(1)}_{os}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha|^{2},

\displaystyle\|q_{2}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}^{2}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\|\partial_{Y}\mbox{div}_{\alpha}(u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||\alpha|^{3},

which along with Proposition
7.1
implies

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon||\alpha|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|,

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}.

In particular, we have

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}},

and

\displaystyle|u_{st}^{(1)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|,\quad|v_{st}^{(1)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}.

Hence by using Lemma
8.1
repeatedly, we infer that for any
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\|v_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j-1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\rho^{(j)}_{os}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|\|u_{os}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|v_{os}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j-1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4},

and

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j)}_{st}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\log c_{i}|^{4}\|v_{st}^{(j-1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}.

Therefore, for
j\geq 2
,
(\rho^{(j)},u^{(j)},v^{(j)})=(\rho^{(j)}_{os}+\rho^{(j)}_{st},u^{(j)}_{os}+u^{(j)}_{st},v^{(j)}_{os}+u^{(j)}_{st})
satisfies

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

\displaystyle\|u^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

\displaystyle\|v^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

and

\displaystyle|u^{(j)}(0)|\leq C\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4},\quad|v^{(j)}(0)|\leq C\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2}|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{4}.

Thus, we may define

\displaystyle\rho^{s}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\rho^{(j)},\quad u^{s}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}u^{(j)},\quad v^{s}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}v^{(j)},

which satisfies the desired properties by Proposition
6.2
