and the above estimates.
∎

Next we construct the fast mode of the linearized CNS system (
8.1
).

Proposition 8.3
.

Let
(\rho_{f},u_{f},v_{f})
be the fast solution to the homogenous quasi-incompressible system constructed in Proposition
6.2
. Then there exists a fast solution
(\rho^{f},u^{f},v^{f})\in\mathcal{Z}\times\tilde{\mathcal{X}}\times\tilde{\mathcal{X}}
to the system (
8.1
) such that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\rho^{f}-\rho_{f}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u^{f}-u_{f}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+\|v^{f}-v_{f}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle u^{f}(0)=

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5}),

\displaystyle v^{f}(0)=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\alpha\big(\psi_{a}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|+|\alpha|)|\log c_{i}|^{5})\big).

Proof.

Since the proof is similar to Proposition
8.2
, we only present a sketch of the proof.

We define
(\rho^{(1)},u^{(1)},v^{(1)})=(\rho^{(1)}_{os}+\rho^{(1)}_{st},u^{(1)}_{os}+u^{(1)}_{st},v^{(1)}_{os}+u^{(1)}_{st})
, where
(\rho_{os}^{(1)},u_{os}^{(1)},v_{os}^{(1)})=(\rho_{f},u_{f},v_{f})
and
(\rho_{st}^{(1)},u_{st}^{(1)},v_{st}^{(1)})
is the solution to the system (
7.1
) constructed in Proposition
7.1
with the source term
(0,q^{(1)}_{1},q^{(1)}_{2})
. The definition of
(q^{(1)}_{1},q^{(1)}_{2})
is given in (
8.2
) and (
8.3
).
By Proposition
6.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|q_{1}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\alpha||\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|q_{2}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}+\|\partial_{Y}q_{2}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\beta_{r}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\alpha|^{2}|\log c_{i}|,

which along with Proposition
7.1
implies

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|u^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}^{2}v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{Y}v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}+\|v^{(1)}_{st}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\beta_{r}}{2}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

In particular, we have

\displaystyle\|\rho_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|u_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}+\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,

and

\displaystyle|u_{st}^{(1)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|,\quad|v_{st}^{(1)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{3}}|\log c_{i}|.

Recall that for
j\geq 2
,
(\rho^{(j)},u^{(j)},v^{(j)})=(\rho^{(j)}_{os}+\rho^{(j)}_{st},u^{(j)}_{os}+u^{(j)}_{st},v^{(j)}_{os}+u^{(j)}_{st})
. By a similar calculation as in the proof of Proposition
8.2
, it holds that for any
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\|\rho^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{Z}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon||\log c_{i}|^{5}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

\displaystyle\|u^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

\displaystyle\|v^{(j)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4})^{j-2}\|v_{st}^{(1)}\|_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5}\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2},

and

\displaystyle|u^{(j)}(0)|\leq C\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5},\quad|v^{(j)}(0)|\leq C\left(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{4}\right)^{j-2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{4}{3}}|\log c_{i}|^{5}.

Then we define

\displaystyle\rho^{f}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\rho^{(j)},\quad u^{f}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}u^{(j)},\quad v^{f}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}v^{(j)},

which satisfies the desired properties by Proposition
6.2
and the above estimates.
∎

9.
Dispersion relation and T-S waves

In this section, we construct the solution
(\rho,u,v)
to the homogeneous linearized compressible NS system:

(9.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)\rho+\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})u+\mathrm{i}\alpha\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)u\\
&\qquad-(\mathrm{i}\alpha m^{-2}+\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}^{2}U_{s})\rho-v\partial_{Y}U_{s}=0,\\
&\sqrt{\nu}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})v+\lambda\sqrt{\nu}\partial_{Y}\mathrm{div}_{\alpha}(u,v)-\mathrm{i}\alpha(U_{s}-c)v-m^{-2}\partial_{Y}\rho=0,\end{aligned}\right.

together with non-slip boundary condition

\displaystyle u(0)=v(0)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}u(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}v(Y)=0.

In Proposition
8.2
and Proposition
8.3
, we have constructed two solutions
(\rho^{s},u^{s},v^{s})
and
(\rho^{f},u^{f},v^{f})
to the homogeneous linearized compressible NS system with the boundary condition at the infinity

\displaystyle\lim_{Y\to\infty}u^{s}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}v^{s}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}u^{f}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}v^{f}(Y)=0.

However, these two solutions do not match non-slip boundary condition. To this end, we find the solution with the form

\displaystyle(\rho,u,v)=C_{s}(\rho^{s},u^{s},v^{s})+C_{f}(\rho^{f},u^{f},v^{f})

with

\displaystyle C_{s}u^{s}(0)+C_{f}u^{f}(0)=0\text{ and }C_{s}v^{s}(0)+C_{f}v^{f}(0)=0.

The existence of
(C_{s},C_{f})
is guaranteed by the following dispersion relation:

(9.2)

\displaystyle\frac{u^{s}(0)}{v^{s}(0)}=\frac{u^{f}(0)}{v^{f}(0)}.

First of all, to ensure the existence of solutions
(\rho^{s},u^{s},v^{s})
and
(\rho^{f},u^{f},v^{f})
, we require
(c,\alpha)\in\mathbb{H}
, where we recall that

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}=\mathbb{H}_{1}\cap\{(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}:|\alpha|\sim|c|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq c_{i}\leq c_{1}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\text{ with }0<c_{0}<c_{1}\ll 1\}

with

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}_{1}=\big\{(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}:(|\alpha|+|c|)|\log c_{i}|\ll 1,\alpha_{r}\geq C_{0}|\alpha_{i}|,c_{i}\geq c_{0}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\text{ and }0<c_{0}\ll 1\ll C_{0}\big\}.

According to Proposition
8.2
and Proposition
8.3
, we find that (
9.2
) is equivalent to

(9.3)

\displaystyle c-\frac{\beta}{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)(1-m^{2})}+\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{R}_{d}(\alpha,c)=0.

Notice that

\displaystyle A_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}=(1-m^{2}(1-c)^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}=(1-m^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

which along with the definition of
\beta=\alpha A^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}
shows that (
9.3
) is equivalent to

(9.4)

\displaystyle c-\frac{\alpha}{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)(1-m^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)=0,

where
\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)
is smooth on
c
and analytic on
\alpha
. Moreover, it holds that for any
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}
,

(9.5)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\varepsilon||,\quad|\partial_{\alpha_{r}}\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)|+|\partial_{\alpha_{i}}\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)|\leq C,\\
&|\partial_{c_{r}}\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)|+|\partial_{c_{i}}\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\varepsilon||.\end{split}

Proposition 9.1
.

Let
m<1
and
0<\nu\leq\nu(m)\ll 1
. Suppose that
(c,\alpha)\in\mathbb{H}
. There exist
1\ll A_{0}<B_{0}
such that for any
\alpha\in\mathbb{G}=\{\alpha\in\mathbb{C}:\alpha_{r}=A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}\text{ with }A\in(A_{0},B_{0}),|\alpha_{i}|\leq\gamma\alpha_{r}\text{ with }\gamma\ll 1\}
, the solution
c(\alpha)
to (
9.4
) satisfies

\displaystyle c_{r}=U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha_{r}+o(\alpha),\quad 0<c_{i}-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha_{i}\sim A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

In particular, for any
\alpha_{r}=A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
with
A\in(A_{0},B_{0})
, there holds

•

there exists
\alpha^{0}=\alpha_{r}+\mathrm{i}\alpha^{0}_{i}\in\mathbb{G}
with
\alpha_{i}^{0}<0
such that

\displaystyle\alpha^{0}c(\alpha^{0})\in\mathbb{R}\text{ and }|\alpha_{i}^{0}|\sim\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

•

there exists
c(\alpha_{r})
such that
c_{i}(\alpha_{r})>0
and
|c_{i}(\alpha_{r})|\sim\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
.

Proof.

Since
|\alpha|\sim A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|\varepsilon|=\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}|\alpha|^{-1}
, we know that
|\varepsilon|\sim A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{3}{8}}
.
We first rewrite (
9.4
) as follows

\displaystyle c-\frac{\alpha}{U^{\prime}_{s}(0)(1-m^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}=-\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}-\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)=C_{Ai,r}+\mathrm{i}C_{Ai,i}.

By Lemma
A.5
, we know that

\displaystyle 0<C_{Ai,r}\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{3}}c_{r}|^{-\frac{1}{2}}=A^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\frac{3}{16}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

and

\displaystyle 0<C_{Ai,i}\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{3}}c_{r}|^{-\frac{1}{2}}=A^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{\frac{3}{16}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

On the other hand, for large enough
A_{0}
and
B_{0}
, we have that for any
\alpha_{r}\in(A_{0}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}},B_{0}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}})
,

\displaystyle c_{r}=U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+C_{Ai,r}\sim A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{4}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}},

which implies that
c_{r}\sim A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.
Moreover, we obtain

\displaystyle 0<c_{i}-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha_{i}\sim A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

In particular, for the case of
\alpha_{i}=0
, we have

\displaystyle 0<c_{i}\sim A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

Now we suppose that
\alpha^{0}=\alpha_{r}+\mathrm{i}\alpha_{i}
with
\alpha_{i}=-\gamma_{0}\alpha_{r}
. Then

\displaystyle c_{i}=-\gamma_{0}U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+C_{Ai,i}.

On the other hand, we notice that
c(\alpha)\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{c_{i}}{c_{r}}=-\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\alpha_{r}}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}=\frac{-\gamma_{0}U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+C_{Ai,i}}{U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+C_{Ai,r}},

which implies

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}=\frac{C_{Ai,i}}{2U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+C_{Ai,r}}>0.

Moreover, we notice that

\displaystyle|\gamma_{0}|\sim A^{-2}\ll 1.

By taking
\alpha^{0}=A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}-\mathrm{i}\gamma_{0}A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
, we finish the proof.
∎

Proposition 9.2
.

Let
m<1
and
0<\nu\leq\nu(m)\ll 1
. Suppose that
(c,\alpha)\in\mathbb{H}
. There exist
1\ll A_{0}<B_{0}
such that for any
\alpha\in\mathbb{G}
, there exists a solution
c(\alpha)
to (
9.4
) with
(c(\alpha),\alpha)\in\mathbb{H}
. Moreover,
c(\alpha)
depends on
\alpha
continuously .

Proof.

We introduce

\mathbb{F}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\big(F_{r}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i}),F_{i}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})\big),

where

\displaystyle F_{r}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\mathrm{Re}\left(c-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha+\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)\right)

\displaystyle F_{i}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\mathrm{Im}\left(c-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha+\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}+\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c)\right).

We need to show the Jacobian determinant of
F_{r}
and
F_{i}
does not equal zero for
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}=\{(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}:\alpha\in\mathbb{G}\}
. For this purpose, by Lemma
A.5
and the definition of
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(j,Y)
, we have that for
j=1,2
,

\displaystyle\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}=\kappa^{-1}U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}+\mathcal{O}(|c|^{2}).

By our definition of
\eta^{La}(Y)
, we know that

\displaystyle\eta^{La}(0;c_{r},c_{i})=-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(c_{r}+\mathrm{i}c_{i})+\mathcal{O}(|c|^{2}),

and smoothly depends on
c_{r}
and
c_{i}
. Therefore, we obtain

(9.6)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{c_{r}}\left(\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}\right)=-1+\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(|c|),\\
&\partial_{c_{i}}\left(\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}\right)=-\mathrm{i}+\mathrm{i}\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(|c|).\end{split}

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{c_{r}}F_{r}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\mathrm{Re}\left(\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

\displaystyle\partial_{c_{r}}F_{i}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\mathrm{Im}\left(\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

\displaystyle\partial_{c_{r}}F_{i}(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=-\mathrm{Im}\left(\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

\displaystyle\partial_{c_{i}}F(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=\mathrm{Re}\left(\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(|c|).

Hence, the Jacobian determinant
J(F_{r},F_{i})(\alpha;c_{r},c_{i})
of
F_{r}
and
F_{i}
satisfies

\displaystyle J(F_{r},F_{i})(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{O}(|c|).

And by Lemma
A.1
, we know that for any
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right|\sim 1.

Therefore, for
\nu\ll 1
, we obtain that for any
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
,

(9.7)

\displaystyle\begin{split}J(F_{r},F_{i})(\alpha_{r},\alpha_{i};c_{r},c_{i})=&\left|\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{O}(|c|)\\
\geq&C(1-|c|)\geq\frac{1}{2}C>0.\end{split}

On the other hand, since
F_{r},F_{i}
are both analytic on
\alpha
, for any fixed
\nu
, we have

(9.8)

\displaystyle|\partial_{\alpha}F_{r}(\alpha;c_{r},c_{i})|+|\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha;c_{r},c_{i})|\leq C,\quad\forall\alpha\in\mathbb{G}.

We denote

\displaystyle\mathbb{A}(c^{(j)})=\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}\text{ with taking }c=c^{(j)}.

We now show that there exists a
(\alpha^{0},c^{0})\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
such that
\mathbb{F}(\alpha^{0};c_{r}^{0},c_{i}^{0})=0
. Let
\alpha=A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
with
A\gg 1
. We take
c^{(0)}=U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha
, and for
j\geq 1
,

\displaystyle c^{(j)}=U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha-\mathbb{A}(c^{(j-1)})-\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c^{(j-1)}).

By Lemma
A.5
, we know that

\displaystyle\mathbb{A}(c^{(0)})=-A^{-1}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{4}}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+\mathcal{O}(A^{-\frac{4}{3}})\nu^{\frac{1}{8}},

which implies

\displaystyle c^{(1)}=\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(U_{s}^{\prime}(0)(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}A+A^{-1}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(A^{-\frac{4}{3}})\nu^{\frac{1}{8}},

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}(c^{(1)})=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}+\mathcal{O}(A^{-\frac{4}{3}})\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

Hence,
(\alpha,c^{(1)})\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
.
Hence, by the inductive argument and Lemma
A.5
, we can obtain that for any
j\geq 1
,
(\alpha,c^{(j)})\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
. Moreover, again by Lemma
A.5
and (
9.6
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{c_{r}}\left(\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}\right)\right|+\left|\partial_{c_{i}}\left(\frac{U_{s}^{\prime}(0)\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}\right)\right|\leq CA^{-\frac{4}{3}}.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
j\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|c^{(j+1)}-c^{(j)}|\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-\frac{4}{3}}|c^{(j)}-c^{(j-1)}|+C|\varepsilon\log|\varepsilon|||c^{(j)}-c^{(j-1)}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-\frac{4}{3}}|c^{(j)}-c^{(j-1)}|.

Since
A\gg 1
, there exists
c^{0}
such that
\lim_{j\to\infty}c^{(j)}=c^{0}
and
c^{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle c^{0}=U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha-\mathbb{A}(c^{0})-\mathcal{R}(\alpha,c^{(0)}).

Hence,
(\alpha,c_{r}^{(0)},c_{i}^{(0)})
is a zero point of
\mathbb{F}(\alpha;c_{r},c_{i})
, which along with (
9.7
) and (
9.8
) implies that for
\alpha\in\mathbb{G}
, there exists a unique
c(\alpha)
solving (
9.4
) with
(c(\alpha),\alpha)\in\mathbb{H}
. Moreover,
c(\alpha)
depends on
\alpha
continuously.
∎

From the above two propositions, we conclude the following result.

Theorem 9.3
.

Let
m<1
. Suppose that
U_{s}(Y)
satisfies the structure assumption (
1.5
). Then there exist
0<\nu(m)\ll 1
and
1\ll A_{0}<B_{0}
such that for any
\nu\leq\nu(m)
, the following statement holds true: for any
\alpha\in\mathbb{G}
, we can find
c(\alpha)
such that there exists a solution
(\rho,u,v)\in W^{1,\infty}\times W^{2,\infty}\times W^{2,\infty}
to the system (
1.3
) and (
1.4
). Moreover, such
c,\alpha
satisfy the following properties

\displaystyle c_{r}=U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha_{r}+o(\alpha),\quad 0<c_{i}-U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{-1}(1-m^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha_{i}\sim A^{-1}\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

In particular, for any
\alpha_{r}=A\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
with
A\in(A_{0},B_{0})
we have the following results:

•

there exists
\alpha^{0}=\alpha_{r}+\mathrm{i}\alpha^{0}_{i}\in\mathbb{G}
with
\alpha_{i}^{0}<0
such that

\displaystyle\alpha^{0}c(\alpha^{0})\in\mathbb{R}\text{ and }|\alpha_{i}^{0}|\sim\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}.

•

there exists
c(\alpha_{r})
such that
c_{i}(\alpha_{r})>0
and
|c_{i}(\alpha_{r})|\sim\nu^{\frac{1}{8}}
.

Appendix A
The Airy function

Let
Ai(y)
be the Airy function, which is a nontrivial solution of
f^{\prime\prime}-yf=0
. We have the following asymptotic formula: for
|\arg z|\leq\pi-\delta
with
\delta>0
and
|z|\geq M
for some large
M
,

(A.1)

\displaystyle\partial^{k}_{z}Ai(z)=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}z^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}z^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+R(z)),\quad R(z)=\mathcal{O}(z^{-\frac{3}{2}}),\quad k=0,1,2.

We denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(1,z)=-\int_{z}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}t)dt,\quad\mathcal{A}(2,z)=-\int_{z}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,t)dt,

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}(1,z)=\int_{0}^{z}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}t)dt,\quad\mathcal{B}(2,z)=\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{B}(1,t)dt,

and

\displaystyle A_{0}(z)=\int_{\mathrm{e}^{{\mathrm{i}}(\pi/6-\theta_{0})}z}^{+\infty}Ai(t)\mathrm{d}t=\mathrm{e}^{{i}(\pi/6-\theta_{0})}\int_{z}^{+\infty}Ai(\mathrm{e}^{{\mathrm{i}}(\pi/6-\theta_{0})}t)\mathrm{d}t=-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\mathcal{A}(1,z),

where
|\theta_{0}|\ll 1
.

The following lemma comes from
[
2
]
.

Lemma A.1
.

There exists
\gamma>0
and
\delta_{0}>0
so that for
\mathrm{Im}z\leq\delta_{0}
, we have

(A.2)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\frac{A_{0}^{\prime}(z)}{A_{0}(z)}\right|\lesssim 1+|z|^{\frac{1}{2}},\quad{\rm Re}\frac{A_{0}^{\prime}(z)}{A_{0}(z)}\leq\min(-1/3,-\gamma(1+|z|^{\frac{1}{2}})),\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{A_{0}^{\prime\prime}(z)}{A_{0}(z)}\Big|\leq C(1+|z|).

Lemma A.2
.

Let
\delta_{0}
be the constant in Lemma
A.1
and
|\theta_{0}|\ll 1
. Assume that
\mathrm{Im}z\leq\delta_{0}
and
|z|\geq M
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(1,z)=-e^{-\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}_{1}(z)),

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(2,z)=e^{-\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{3}-2\theta_{0})}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{-\frac{5}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}_{2}(z)),

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}(1,z)=-e^{-\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}_{3}(z)),

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}(2,z)=e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{3}+2\theta_{0})}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{-\frac{5}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}z)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}_{4}(z)),

where
\mathcal{R}_{i}(z)=\mathcal{O}(z^{-\frac{3}{2}})
for
i=1,2,3,4.

Here we omit the proof of this lemma and refer to Lemma
A.4
for the proof in more complicated cases.

Lemma A.3
.

Let
\delta_{0}>0
be the constant in Lemma
A.1
and
|\theta_{0}|\ll 1
. Suppose
-\delta_{0}<\mathrm{Im}Z_{0}<\delta_{0}
,
\mathrm{Re}Z_{0}<0
. There exist
c_{1}>0,M_{1}>0
such that if
|\mathrm{Re}(Y+Z_{0})|>M_{1}\delta_{0}
, then for any
0\leq Z\leq Y
,

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|\leq e^{-c_{1}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Proof.

Notice that

(A.3)

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|=e^{-\frac{2}{3}\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})}.

Thus, it is enough to focus on the estimate about
\mathrm{Re}\big((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)
.

Case 1.
0\leq Z\leq Y\leq-\mathrm{Re}Z_{0}
.
In this case, for large enough
M_{1}
,

\displaystyle|(Z+Z_{0})|\geq|(Y+Z_{0})|,\quad|\mathrm{Re}(Z+Z_{0})|\geq|\mathrm{Re}(Y+Z_{0})|,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}(Y+Z_{0})=\mathrm{Im}(Z+Z_{0}),

\displaystyle\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})\in[\frac{\pi}{2}+\xi_{0},\pi-\xi_{0}],

\displaystyle\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\in[-\frac{\pi}{2}+\xi_{0},-\xi_{0}],

where
\xi_{0}\in(0,\frac{\pi}{4})
. Then we infer that for any
0\leq Z\leq Y\leq-\mathrm{Re}Z_{0}
,

\displaystyle\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\leq 0,

\displaystyle-\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))=\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})))\geq c_{0}>0,

and

\displaystyle\left|\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\right|\geq\left|\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0}}(Y+Z_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}))\right|.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})

\displaystyle=\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))+\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))+\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle=\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))-\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq c_{0}(|Z+Z_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}}-|Y+Z_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}})\geq{c}_{0}(|Z+Z_{0}|-|Y+Z_{0}|)(|Z+Z_{0}|+|Y+Y_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq c_{0}|Y-Z|(|Z+Z_{0}|+|Y+Y_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

which along with (
A.3
) implies that for any
0\leq Z\leq Y\leq\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
,

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|\leq e^{-c_{1}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Case 2.
0\leq Z\leq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})\leq Y
.
We first notice that

\displaystyle\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})))\geq 0,\quad\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\geq 0.

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})

\displaystyle=\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))

\displaystyle\quad+\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}\geq 0.

Moreover, by the assumption on
|Y+Z_{0}|
, we have

(A.4)

\displaystyle\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\geq c_{0}.

Hence, for any
0\leq Z\leq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
and
Y\geq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})\geq c_{0}\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Then we obtain that for any
0\leq Z\leq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
,
Y\geq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
and
|Z+Z_{0}|\leq|Y+Z_{0}|
,

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{2}{3}c_{0}|Y+Z_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{-c_{1}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

On the other hand, for any
0\leq Z\leq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
,
Y\geq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
and
|Z+Z_{0}|\geq|Y+Z_{0}|
, we have

\displaystyle\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\geq c_{0},

which implies

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})\geq c_{0}\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Therefore, we conclude that for any
0\leq Z\leq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
,
Y\geq-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})
and
|Z+Z_{0}|\geq|Y+Z_{0}|

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{2}{3}c_{0}|Z+Z_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{-c_{1}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Case 3.
0<-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})\leq Z\leq Y
.
In this case, we first notice that

\displaystyle\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))>0,

\displaystyle-\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))=\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})))<0,

and

\displaystyle|Z+Z_{0}|\leq|Y+Z_{0}|,\quad\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))\geq\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})),

which implies

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})

\displaystyle=\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))+\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle=\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))-\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))-\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq(\left|(Y+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}}-\left|(Z+Z_{0})\right|^{\frac{3}{2}})\cos(\arg((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}))>0.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
-\mathrm{Re}(Z_{0})\leq Z\leq Y
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}((e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}+(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}})\geq c_{0}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Thus, we arrive at

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Y+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}(Z+Z_{0}))^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|\leq Ce^{-c_{1}|Y-Z|(|Y+Z_{0}|+|Z+Z_{0}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

∎

Lemma A.4
.

Let
|\alpha|,|\varepsilon|,|c|\ll 1
and
c_{r}>0
. Suppose that
0<\delta_{0}\ll 1\leq M
are the constants in Lemma
A.1
. Then there holds

(1)

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{-\theta_{0}i}}{2\sqrt{\pi}}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{3}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-1}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{O}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}})),

\displaystyle A_{1}(2,Y)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-1}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-2}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{5}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{O}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}})),

\displaystyle A_{2}(1,Y)=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\pi}e^{-\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{1}{3}}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-1}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{O}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}})),

\displaystyle A_{2}(2,Y)=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\pi}e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{3}+2\theta_{0})}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-2}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{5}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{O}(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}})).

(2)

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle|A_{1}(1,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}},\quad|A_{1}(2,Y)|\leq C,

\displaystyle|A_{2}(1,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},\quad|A_{2}(2,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Proof.

By the definition of
A_{1}(1,Y)
, we can write

(A.5)

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)=\mathrm{i}e^{-2\theta_{0}i}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ.

Case 1.
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
.
By the definition of
\eta^{La}(Y)
and Lemma
4.1
, we know
\mathcal{N}^{+}\subset[Y_{c}+2\kappa^{-1}M,+\infty)
and
\eta^{La}=\eta_{out}^{La}+i\eta_{i}^{La}
. By (
A.1
), we have that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

(A.6)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}&Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\\
=&\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+R(Z))dZ\\
=&\frac{-1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)\Big)^{-1}\partial_{Z}\Big(e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)dZ\\
&+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}R(Z)dZ\\
=&\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\\
&+\Bigg(\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Z}\Big[\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-1}\Big]e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}dZ\\
&+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}R(Z)dZ\Bigg)\\
=&\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+I_{1}.\end{split}

Notice that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

(A.7)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}=&\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}(Y),\end{split}

where

(A.8)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}(Y)=\int_{Y}^{+\infty}(\mathcal{T}(Y,Z)+\mathcal{S}(Y,Z))e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}dZ

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}(Y,Z)=\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\partial_{Z}\Big[\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-1}\Big],

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}(Y,Z)=\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}R(Z).

For any
Y\leq Z
and
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
, we have
\eta^{La}=\eta_{out}^{La}+i\eta_{i}^{La}\sim\eta_{out}^{La}
,
which along with Lemma
A.6
implies that for any
Y\leq Z
and
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}(Y,Z)|\leq

\displaystyle|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\Big(|\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{7}{4}}+|\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)||\partial_{Z}\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-2}|\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)||\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-\frac{7}{4}}\leq C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{-1}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|,

where we used the facts that

\displaystyle|\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|\leq C(1+|Z-Y_{c}|)^{-\frac{4}{3}}\leq C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-2},

\displaystyle|\partial_{Z}\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|^{-2}\leq C(1+|Z-Y_{c}|)^{\frac{2}{3}}\leq C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Z)|.

Similarly, we obtain that for any
Y\leq Z
and
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{S}(Y,Z)|\leq C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{-1}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

(A.9)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{R}_{1}(Y)|\leq&C|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{-1}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|dZ.\end{split}

On the other hand, by Lemma
A.3
, for any
0\leq Y\leq Z
and
Y,Z\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|dZ

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-C_{1}\kappa|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)|(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Y}^{+\infty}e^{-C_{2}\kappa|\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Z)||\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ

\displaystyle\leq C\kappa^{-1}|\kappa\eta^{La}_{out}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}_{out}(Y)|^{-1}.

Therefore, according to (
A.8
), we obtain that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{1}(Y)|\leq C\kappa^{-\frac{3}{2}}|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|^{-1}|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)||\eta^{La}_{out}(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}_{out}(Y)|^{-1}\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}},

which along with (
A.6
) and (
A.7
) deduces that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}_{1}(Y)).

This proves the first statement of (1) for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{+}
by using (
A.5
).

Case 2.
Y\in\mathcal{N}.
In this case, there exists
Y_{+}\sim Y_{c}+2M\kappa^{-1}
such that
\mathcal{N}^{+}=[Y_{+},+\infty)
. Hence,

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{+\infty}

\displaystyle Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ=\int_{Y}^{Y_{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ.

By the property of the Airy function, we know that for any
Z\in[Y,Y_{+}]
,

\displaystyle\Big|Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\Big|\leq C.

Notice that

\displaystyle|Y_{+}-Y_{c}|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}M,

which implies that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle|Y_{+}-Y|\leq|Y_{+}-Y_{c}|+|Y_{c}-Y|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}M.

Then we obtain that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{Y}^{Y_{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\Big|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

According to results in case 1, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\mathcal{N}^{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|.

By Lemma
4.1
, we know that

(A.10)

\displaystyle\left|\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\Big)^{-1}\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

Since
|\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+}))|=|\kappa\eta^{La}_{i}|<\delta_{0}
and
|\mathrm{Re}(\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+}))|\sim M\gg 1
, we know that

\displaystyle\arg\left(\left(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\in[\frac{\pi}{8},\frac{3\pi}{8}],

which implies

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}})>0.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right|=e^{-\frac{2}{3}\mathrm{Re}(\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{+})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}})}\leq 1,

which along with (
A.10
) implies that

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\mathcal{N}^{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\Big|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

This shows that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle|A_{1}(1,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}.

Case 3.
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
.
We first point out that if
|\kappa Y_{c}|\leq M
, then
\mathcal{N}^{-}=\emptyset
. Hence, we only need to consider the case of
\kappa Y_{c}>M
. Then there exists
Y_{-}>0
such that
\mathcal{N}^{-}=[0,Y_{-}]
and
Y_{-}\sim Y_{c}-2\kappa^{-1}M\ll 1
. Hence, we can write that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ=

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ+\int_{\mathcal{N}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta(Z))dZ

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ.

According to results for the above two cases, we know that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathcal{N}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\right|+\left|\int_{\mathcal{N}^{+}}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

On the other hand, we notice that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}

\displaystyle Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+R(Z))dZ

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-1}\partial_{Z}\Big(e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)dZ

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}R(Z)dZ

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y_{-})\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}\partial_{Z}\Big[\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-1}\Big]e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}dZ

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\Big)^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}R(Z)dZ\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle I_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)=\Big(\frac{\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)}{\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})}\Big)^{\frac{3}{4}}\frac{\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)}{\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y_{-})}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

By the definition of
\eta^{La}(Y)
and Lemma
A.3
, we have that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}

i.e.
Y\in[0,Y_{-}]
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)\right|\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C_{1}\kappa(\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta(Y_{-}))(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

If
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
such that
|\eta^{La}(Y)|\leq 2|\eta^{La}(Y_{-})|\leq 2|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}M
, then we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)|\leq C\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})|^{-\frac{3}{2}}\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

If
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
such that
|\eta^{La}(Y)|\geq 2|\eta^{La}(Y_{-})|
, then we have

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C_{1}\kappa(\eta^{La}(Y)-\eta^{La}(Y_{-}))(|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y_{-})|^{\frac{1}{2}})}\leq

\displaystyle C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C_{3}|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)|\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

By a similar argument in case 1, we can show that there exists
\mathcal{R}_{3}(Y)
such that

(A.11)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}=&\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-1}e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\mathcal{R}_{3}(Y),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{3}(Y)|\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{-\theta_{0}i}}{2\sqrt{\pi}}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{3}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-1}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big(1+\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)+\mathcal{R}_{3}(Y)\big)

with

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{2}(Y)|+|\mathcal{R}_{3}(Y)|\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

By gathering the results for the above three cases, we conclude that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
, there exists
\mathcal{R}(Y)
such that

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{-\theta_{0}i}}{2\sqrt{\pi}}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{3}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}U_{s}^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(Y)^{-1}\Big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\Big)^{-\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{2}{3}\big(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)\big)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+\mathcal{R}(Y)),

where

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}(Y)|\leq C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{2}},

and for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,

\displaystyle|A_{1}(1,Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}.

The other parts of the lemma can be proved in a similar way. Let‘s leave the proof to the interested readers.
∎

Lemma A.5
.

Let
|\varepsilon|,|c|,|\theta_{0}|\ll 1\ll\kappa
and
c_{r}>0
. Suppose
|\kappa\eta_{i}^{La}|<\delta_{0}
. Then there holds

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)-\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\right|\leq C\kappa^{-1}(|c|+\kappa^{-1}),

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)-\kappa^{-2}\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\right|\leq C\kappa^{-2}(\kappa^{-1}+|c|),

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}-\kappa^{-1}\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}\right|\leq C\kappa^{-1}(\kappa^{-1}+|c|).

Moreover, if
|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|>M
, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}=

\displaystyle-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\theta_{0}}{2})}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}+o(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}),

and

\displaystyle\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)^{2}}=1+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{3}{2}}).

Proof.

Recall that

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)=

\displaystyle-A(0)\int_{0}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))dZ

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\eta^{La}(Z)^{\prime}(\eta^{La}(Z)^{\prime})^{-1}dZ+\mathcal{O}(|c|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(0)^{-1}-\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta^{La}(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{2}}dZ+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{-2}).

By Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\partial_{Y}\eta^{La}(0)^{-1}=\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta^{La}(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{2}}dZ\right|\leq C\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))|dZ\leq C\kappa^{-1}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)-\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\right|\leq C\kappa^{-1}(|c|+\kappa^{-1}).

By the definition of
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y)
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)=-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,Z)dZ

\displaystyle\quad=-\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{-1}dZ+\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}A(Z)\int_{Z}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(\xi))\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\eta^{La}(\xi)}{\partial_{\xi}\eta^{La}(\xi)^{2}}d\xi dZ

\displaystyle\qquad+m^{2}\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}(U_{s}-c)^{2}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\quad=\kappa^{-2}\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(0)^{-2}-2\kappa^{-2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta^{La}(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{3}}dZ

\displaystyle\qquad+\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}A(Z)\int_{Z}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(\xi))\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\eta^{La}(\xi)}{\partial_{\xi}\eta^{La}(\xi)^{2}}d\xi dZ+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{-3}).

Notice that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(0)^{-2}=\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(Y))+\mathcal{O}(|c|),

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(Z))\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta^{La}(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta^{La}(Z)^{3}}dZ\right|\leq C\kappa^{-1},

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\int_{Z}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(\xi))\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\eta^{La}(\xi)}{\partial_{\xi}\eta^{La}(\xi)^{2}}d\xi dZ\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\int_{0}^{Y_{+}}\int_{Z}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(\xi))\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\eta^{La}(\xi)}{\partial_{\xi}\eta^{La}(\xi)^{2}}d\xi dZ\right|+\left|\int_{Y_{+}}^{+\infty}\int_{Z}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(\xi))\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\eta^{La}(\xi)}{\partial_{\xi}\eta^{La}(\xi)^{2}}d\xi dZ\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\kappa^{-1}\int_{0}^{Y^{+}}dZ+C\kappa^{-1}\int_{Y_{+}}^{+\infty}e^{-C|\kappa\eta^{La}(Y)|}dY

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\kappa^{-2}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)-\kappa^{-2}\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\right|\leq C\kappa^{-2}(\kappa^{-1}+|c|),

which implies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}-\kappa^{-1}\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}\right|\leq C\kappa^{-1}(\kappa^{-1}+|c|).

If
|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|\geq M
, then by Lemma
A.2
, we first have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}=-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\left(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0)\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1+\mathcal{R}(\kappa\eta^{La}(0))),

where

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}(\kappa\eta^{La}(0))|\leq CM^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ll 1.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0)\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}=

\displaystyle\left(-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa Y_{c}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}|c|^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\kappa c_{r}|^{-\frac{1}{2}}U^{\prime}_{s}(0)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{5\pi}{12}+\frac{\theta_{0}}{2})}+\mathcal{O}(\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}|c|^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}=-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\theta_{0}}{2})}U_{s}^{\prime}(0)^{\frac{1}{3}}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{6}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}+o(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{6}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

This shows that for the case
|\kappa\eta^{La}(0)|\geq M
,

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)}=

\displaystyle\kappa^{-1}\frac{\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}+o(\kappa^{-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\theta_{0}}{2})}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}+o(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\kappa^{-1}).

According to the above arguments, we find that

\displaystyle\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)^{2}}=\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c|).

And by Lemma
A.2
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\mathcal{A}(2,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))}{\mathcal{A}(1,\kappa\eta^{La}(0))^{2}}=1+\mathcal{O}(|\kappa c_{r}|^{-\frac{3}{2}}).

Therefore, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}(\frac{\pi}{6}-\theta_{0})}\kappa\eta^{La}(0))\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,0)}{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,0)^{2}}=1+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{r}^{-\frac{3}{2}}).

∎

For
\eta_{out}^{La}
defined by (
4.1
), it is easy to show the following lemma.

Lemma A.6
.

Suppose that
U_{s}
satisfies the structure assumption (
1.5
). Then there exists a positive constant
L\leq 1
such that
if
0<|Y-Y_{c}|\leq L
, then

\displaystyle|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)-(Y-Y_{c})|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|^{2},\quad|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)-1|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|,\quad|\partial_{Y}^{2}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\leq C,

and if
|Y-Y_{c}|\geq L
, then

\displaystyle C^{-1}|Y-Y_{c}|^{\frac{2}{3}}\leq|\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|^{\frac{2}{3}},

\displaystyle C^{-1}|Y-Y_{c}|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\leq|\partial_{Y}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|^{-\frac{1}{3}},

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}^{2}\eta_{out}^{La}(Y)|\leq C|Y-Y_{c}|^{-\frac{4}{3}}.
